Implement the following plan:

# Phase 4: Join-Ready Export (CIRCE, FHIR Group, Evaluation SQL)

## Context

Phases 1–3b are complete: the pipeline extracts criteria, grounds entities via dual grounding (ToolUniverse + OMOP), builds expression trees with AND/OR/NOT logic, normalizes units/values, and resolves unknown ordinal scales. The `atomic_criteria`, `composite_criteria`, and `criterion_relationships` tables are populated with concept-grounded, unit-normalized rows.

The goal is to make this data **join-ready** — optimally indexed and exportable in standard formats so any future patient matching system, OHDSI Atlas instance, or FHIR-based matcher can consume it directly.

This phase adds **three read-only export endpoints** to the api-service and an **Alembic migration** for index optimization. No changes to the protocol-processor-service pipeline.

## Files

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `api-service/src/api_service/exporters/__init__.py` | **NEW** — `ProtocolExportData` dataclass + shared DB loader |
| `api-service/src/api_service/exporters/circe_builder.py` | **NEW** — CIRCE CohortExpression JSON builder |
| `api-service/src/api_service/exporters/fhir_group_builder.py` | **NEW** — FHIR Group resource builder |
| `api-service/src/api_service/exporters/evaluation_sql_builder.py` | **NEW** — OMOP CDM evaluation SQL generator |
| `api-service/src/api_service/exports.py` | **NEW** — FastAPI router with 3 GET endpoints |
| `api-service/src/api_service/main.py` | **MODIFY** — import + mount exports router (2 lines) |
| `api-service/alembic/versions/44_01_add_export_indexes.py` | **NEW** — index optimization migration |
| `api-service/tests/test_exports.py` | **NEW** — unit + integration tests |

## Step 1: Shared Data Loader — `exporters/__init__.py`

`ProtocolExportData` dataclass holds everything all three exporters need:

```python
@dataclass
class ProtocolExportData:
    protocol: Protocol
    criteria: list[Criteria]
    atomics: list[AtomicCriterion]
    composites: list[CompositeCriterion]
    relationships: list[CriterionRelationship]
    atomics_by_id: dict[str, AtomicCriterion]
    composites_by_id: dict[str, CompositeCriterion]
    children_by_parent: dict[str, list[CriterionRelationship]]  # sorted by child_sequence
    criteria_by_id: dict[str, Criteria]
```

`load_protocol_export_data(db, protocol_id)` queries the active (non-archived) batch and all associated records. Returns `None` if not found.

## Step 2: CIRCE Builder — `exporters/circe_builder.py`

`build_circe_export(data) -> dict` produces OHDSI CIRCE CohortExpression JSON.

**Key mappings:**
| Our data | CIRCE equivalent |
|----------|-----------------|
| `omop_concept_id` (or `entity_concept_id` fallback) | ConceptSet item with `includeDescendants: true` |
| `entity_domain` | CIRCE domain table (condition→ConditionOccurrence, measurement→Measurement, drug→DrugExposure, procedure→ProcedureOccurrence) |
| `relation_operator` + `value_numeric` | Value attribute filter (Op: gt/gte/lt/lte/eq) |
| `unit_concept_id` | Unit filter |
| `negation=true` | OccurrenceCount=0 (absence) |
| `inclusion_exclusion="inclusion"` | AdditionalCriteria |
| `inclusion_exclusion="exclusion"` | CensoringCriteria |
| Composite AND/OR | CriteriaGroup Type="ALL"/"ANY" |

Walks `Criteria.structured_criterion` JSONB tree recursively. The tree uses `ExpressionNode.type` (AND/OR/NOT/ATOMIC) and `ExpressionNode.atomic_criterion_id` (FK to atomic_criteria.id).

## Step 3: FHIR Group Builder — `exporters/fhir_group_builder.py`

`build_fhir_group_export(data) -> dict` produces FHIR R4 Group resource (EBM IG).

**Key mappings:**
| Our data | FHIR equivalent |
|----------|----------------|
| `entity_concept_system` | System URI (snomed→`http://snomed.info/sct`, loinc→`http://loinc.org`) |
| `value_numeric` + `unit_text` + `relation_operator` | `valueQuantity` with comparator (ge/gt/le/lt) |
| `value_text` | `valueCodeableConcept` |
| `negation` or exclusion | `exclude: true` |
| AND | Flat characteristics, `combinationMethod: "all-of"` |
| OR | Nested contained Group, `combinationMethod: "any-of"` |
| NOT | Inverted `exclude` flag |

## Step 4: Evaluation SQL Builder — `exporters/evaluation_sql_builder.py`

`build_evaluation_sql(data) -> str` generates ready-to-run SQL for OMOP CDM v5.4.

Structure:
1. **Per-atomic CTEs** using `concept_ancestor` for descendant expansion
2. **Domain-specific evaluation**: measurement→`value_as_number`, demographics→`year_of_birth` age calc, condition/drug/procedure→existence check
3. **Final eligibility query**: `SELECT person_id FROM person WHERE EXISTS(inclusion) AND NOT EXISTS(exclusion)`

SQL returned as text (not executed). Concept IDs validated as integers before interpolation.

## Step 5: FastAPI Router — `exports.py`

| Endpoint | Response Type | Content |
|----------|--------------|---------|
| `GET /protocols/{id}/export/circe` | JSON (CirceExportResponse) | CohortExpression + stats |
| `GET /protocols/{id}/export/fhir-group` | JSON (FhirGroupExportResponse) | Group resource + stats |
| `GET /protocols/{id}/export/evaluation-sql` | PlainTextResponse | SQL text |

All return 404 for missing protocol or no structured criteria. Protected by `get_current_user` dependency.

## Step 6: Mount Router — `main.py`

Two lines added alongside existing router imports/mounts:
```python
from api_service.exports import router as exports_router  # noqa: E402
app.include_router(exports_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])
```

## Step 7: Index Migration — `44_01_add_export_indexes.py`

Revises `43_01_unit_value_concept_ids`. Adds indexes for future join patterns:

| Index | Table | Columns |
|-------|-------|---------|
| `ix_atomic_domain_concept` | atomic_criteria | (entity_domain, omop_concept_id) |
| `ix_composite_proto_incl` | composite_criteria | (protocol_id, inclusion_exclusion) |
| `ix_rel_child_criterion` | criterion_relationships | (child_criterion_id) |
| `ix_atomic_crit_incl` | atomic_criteria | (criterion_id, inclusion_exclusion) |

Idempotent — checks existing indexes before creating. Same pattern as `43_01`.

## Step 8: Tests — `tests/test_exports.py`

Uses existing test fixtures from `conftest.py` (`db_session`, `test_client`, `unauthenticated_client`). Creates Protocol + CriteriaBatch + Criteria + AtomicCriterion + CompositeCriterion + CriterionRelationship in SQLite.

**Unit tests (builder functions):**
- CIRCE: concept sets, domain mapping, value/unit filters, negation, AND/OR groups, inclusion vs exclusion, omop_concept_id precedence, empty protocol
- FHIR: characteristics, quantity values, text values, exclusion, OR nesting, NOT inversion, system URIs
- SQL: domain CTEs, measurement value filters, demographics age calc, eligibility query, negation

**Integration tests (via test_client):**
- 200 for each endpoint with valid protocol
- 404 for unknown protocol_id
- 404 for protocol with no structured criteria
- Response stats match actual data

## Verification

1. `uv run ruff check .` in api-service — clean
2. `uv run pytest tests/ -v` in api-service — all tests pass
3. Manual: `curl` each endpoint for a processed protocol (from run_ordinal_e2e.py output), inspect JSON/SQL
4. CIRCE: ConceptSets reference valid OMOP concept_ids, domains match entity_domain
5. FHIR: Group structure matches EBM IG spec
6. SQL: paste into SQL client, verify it parses

{"parentUuid":"f81ca0ae-409a-4275-ad2d-9bf203b16a73","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon","sessionId":"9e308917-11ec-45e0-ba67-9ff5deffb7c1","version":"2.1.47","gitBranch":"feature/major-refactor-langgraph","agentId":"ad5be6a","slug":"shimmering-pondering-sedgewick","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BrLh3TeaJBk8jFJeSzccge","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→\"\"\"Storage client for protocol PDF files.\n     2→\n     3→Supports two backends:\n     4→- GCS (production): Signed URLs for direct browser-to-GCS uploads\n     5→- Local (development): Files stored in uploads/ directory, served by the API\n     6→\n     7→Set USE_LOCAL_STORAGE=1 for local development without GCP service account keys.\n     8→Otherwise requires GCS_BUCKET_NAME and valid GCP credentials.\n     9→\"\"\"\n    10→\n    11→from __future__ import annotations\n    12→\n    13→import hashlib\n    14→import json\n    15→import logging\n    16→import os\n    17→from datetime import timedelta\n    18→from pathlib import Path\n    19→from uuid import uuid4\n    20→\n    21→from shared.resilience import gcs_breaker\n    22→from tenacity import (\n    23→    before_sleep_log,\n    24→    retry,\n    25→    retry_if_exception_type,\n    26→    retry_if_not_exception_type,\n    27→    stop_after_attempt,\n    28→    wait_random_exponential,\n    29→)\n    30→\n    31→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n    32→\n    33→# Local storage directory (relative to repo root)\n    34→_LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR = Path(os.getenv(\"LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR\", \"uploads/protocols\"))\n    35→\n    36→# Shared retry decorator for GCS operations\n    37→# Retry on any Exception EXCEPT ValueError (config errors)\n    38→_gcs_retry = retry(\n    39→    retry=(\n    40→        retry_if_exception_type(Exception) & retry_if_not_exception_type(ValueError)\n    41→    ),\n    42→    stop=stop_after_attempt(3),\n    43→    wait=wait_random_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=10),\n    44→    before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),\n    45→    reraise=True,\n    46→)\n    47→\n    48→# Module-level singleton for GCS client\n    49→_gcs_client = None\n    50→\n    51→\n    52→def _use_local_storage() -> bool:\n    53→    \"\"\"Check if local file storage is enabled.\"\"\"\n    54→    return os.getenv(\"USE_LOCAL_STORAGE\", \"\").strip() in (\"1\", \"true\", \"yes\")\n    55→\n    56→\n    57→def get_gcs_client():\n    58→    \"\"\"Return a cached GCS storage client (singleton).\n    59→\n    60→    Returns:\n    61→        google.cloud.storage.Client or None if unavailable.\n    62→    \"\"\"\n    63→    global _gcs_client  # noqa: PLW0603\n    64→    if _gcs_client is None:\n    65→        try:\n    66→            from google.cloud import storage  # type: ignore[import-untyped]\n    67→\n    68→            _gcs_client = storage.Client()\n    69→            logger.info(\"GCS client initialized successfully\")\n    70→        except Exception:\n    71→            logger.warning(\n    72→                \"Could not initialize GCS client. \"\n    73→                \"GCS operations will fail until GCP credentials are configured.\"\n    74→            )\n    75→            _gcs_client = None\n    76→    return _gcs_client\n    77→\n    78→\n    79→def get_bucket_name() -> str:\n    80→    \"\"\"Read GCS_BUCKET_NAME from environment.\n    81→\n    82→    Returns:\n    83→        The bucket name string.\n    84→\n    85→    Raises:\n    86→        ValueError: If GCS_BUCKET_NAME is not set.\n    87→    \"\"\"\n    88→    bucket_name = os.getenv(\"GCS_BUCKET_NAME\")\n    89→    if not bucket_name:\n    90→        raise ValueError(\n    91→            \"GCS_BUCKET_NAME environment variable is not set. \"\n    92→            \"Set it to your GCS bucket name for protocol storage.\"\n    93→        )\n    94→    return bucket_name\n    95→\n    96→\n    97→def _parse_gcs_uri(gcs_path: str) -> tuple[str, str]:\n    98→    \"\"\"Parse a gs:// URI into (bucket_name, blob_path).\n    99→\n   100→    Args:\n   101→        gcs_path: GCS URI in the form gs://bucket/path/to/blob.\n   102→\n   103→    Returns:\n   104→        Tuple of (bucket_name, blob_path).\n   105→\n   106→    Raises:\n   107→        ValueError: If the URI format is invalid.\n   108→    \"\"\"\n   109→    if not gcs_path.startswith(\"gs://\"):\n   110→        raise ValueError(f\"Invalid GCS URI: {gcs_path}. Must start with gs://\")\n   111→    path = gcs_path[5:]  # Remove \"gs://\"\n   112→    parts = path.split(\"/\", 1)\n   113→    if len(parts) < 2 or not parts[1]:\n   114→        raise ValueError(\n   115→            f\"Invalid GCS URI: {gcs_path}. Must be gs://bucket/path/to/blob\"\n   116→        )\n   117→    return parts[0], parts[1]\n   118→\n   119→\n   120→def generate_upload_url(\n   121→    filename: str,\n   122→    content_type: str = \"application/pdf\",\n   123→    expiration_minutes: int = 15,\n   124→) -> tuple[str, str]:\n   125→    \"\"\"Generate a URL for uploading a file.\n   126→\n   127→    In local mode: returns a local API endpoint URL.\n   128→    In GCS mode: returns a signed GCS PUT URL.\n   129→    \"\"\"\n   130→    if _use_local_storage():\n   131→        return _local_generate_upload_url(filename)\n   132→    return _gcs_generate_upload_url(filename, content_type, expiration_minutes)\n   133→\n   134→\n   135→def set_blob_metadata(gcs_path: str, metadata: dict[str, str]) -> None:\n   136→    \"\"\"Set custom metadata on a stored blob.\"\"\"\n   137→    if _use_local_storage() or gcs_path.startswith(\"local://\"):\n   138→        logger.info(\"Local storage: skipping metadata for %s\", gcs_path)\n   139→        return\n   140→    _gcs_set_blob_metadata(gcs_path, metadata)\n   141→\n   142→\n   143→def generate_download_url(gcs_path: str, expiration_minutes: int = 60) -> str:\n   144→    \"\"\"Generate a URL for downloading a file.\"\"\"\n   145→    if _use_local_storage() or gcs_path.startswith(\"local://\"):\n   146→        return _local_generate_download_url(gcs_path)\n   147→    return _gcs_generate_download_url(gcs_path, expiration_minutes)\n   148→\n   149→\n   150→# --- Local storage backend ---\n   151→\n   152→\n   153→def _local_generate_upload_url(filename: str) -> tuple[str, str]:\n   154→    \"\"\"Generate a local upload URL and file path.\"\"\"\n   155→    blob_id = str(uuid4())\n   156→    blob_path = f\"{blob_id}/{filename}\"\n   157→\n   158→    # Ensure directory exists\n   159→    upload_dir = _LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR / blob_id\n   160→    upload_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n   161→\n   162→    api_port = os.getenv(\"API_PORT\", \"8000\")\n   163→    upload_url = f\"http://localhost:{api_port}/local-upload/{blob_path}\"\n   164→    local_uri = f\"local://{blob_path}\"\n   165→\n   166→    logger.info(\"Local storage: upload URL for %s -> %s\", filename, local_uri)\n   167→    return (upload_url, local_uri)\n   168→\n   169→\n   170→def _local_generate_download_url(gcs_path: str) -> str:\n   171→    \"\"\"Generate a local download URL from a local:// URI.\"\"\"\n   172→    # local://{uuid}/{filename} -> /local-files/{uuid}/{filename}\n   173→    # Also handle legacy local://protocols/ URIs for backward compatibility\n   174→    path = gcs_path.replace(\"local://protocols/\", \"\").replace(\"local://\", \"\")\n   175→    api_port = os.getenv(\"API_PORT\", \"8000\")\n   176→    return f\"http://localhost:{api_port}/local-files/{path}\"\n   177→\n   178→\n   179→def _content_hash(data: bytes) -> str:\n   180→    \"\"\"Compute SHA-256 hex digest of file content for deduplication.\n   181→\n   182→    Args:\n   183→        data: Raw file bytes.\n   184→\n   185→    Returns:\n   186→        SHA-256 hex digest string.\n   187→    \"\"\"\n   188→    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()\n   189→\n   190→\n   191→def local_save_file(blob_path: str, data: bytes) -> None:\n   192→    \"\"\"Save uploaded file to local storage with SHA-256 deduplication.\n   193→\n   194→    Uses a hash-index file (.hash-index.json) in the upload directory to\n   195→    detect duplicate content. If an identical file already exists, creates a\n   196→    symlink to the original rather than storing duplicate bytes.\n   197→\n   198→    Args:\n   199→        blob_path: Relative path within the local upload directory.\n   200→        data: Raw file bytes to store.\n   201→    \"\"\"\n   202→    file_path = _LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR / blob_path\n   203→    file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n   204→\n   205→    content_hash = _content_hash(data)\n   206→    hash_index_path = _LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR / \".hash-index.json\"\n   207→\n   208→    # Load existing hash index or create empty dict\n   209→    hash_index: dict[str, str] = {}\n   210→    if hash_index_path.exists():\n   211→        try:\n   212→            hash_index = json.loads(hash_index_path.read_text())\n   213→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):\n   214→            logger.warning(\"Could not read hash index — starting fresh\")\n   215→\n   216→    if content_hash in hash_index:\n   217→        existing_path = _LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR / hash_index[content_hash]\n   218→        logger.info(\n   219→            \"Dedup: content hash %s... already stored at %s\",\n   220→            content_hash[:8],\n   221→            existing_path,\n   222→        )\n   223→        # Create symlink to existing file (saves disk space)\n   224→        if existing_path.exists() and not file_path.exists():\n   225→            file_path.symlink_to(existing_path)\n   226→        elif not file_path.exists():\n   227→            # Fallback: existing file gone, store normally and update index\n   228→            file_path.write_bytes(data)\n   229→            hash_index[content_hash] = blob_path\n   230→    else:\n   231→        # New content — store file and record in index\n   232→        file_path.write_bytes(data)\n   233→        hash_index[content_hash] = blob_path\n   234→        logger.info(\"Local storage: saved %d bytes to %s\", len(data), file_path)\n   235→\n   236→    # Write updated hash index back to disk\n   237→    try:\n   238→        hash_index_path.write_text(json.dumps(hash_index, indent=2))\n   239→    except OSError:\n   240→        logger.warning(\"Could not write hash index to %s\", hash_index_path)\n   241→\n   242→\n   243→def local_get_file_path(blob_path: str) -> Path | None:\n   244→    \"\"\"Get the local filesystem path for a stored file.\"\"\"\n   245→    file_path = _LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR / blob_path\n   246→    return file_path if file_path.exists() else None\n   247→\n   248→\n   249→# --- GCS backend ---\n   250→\n   251→\n   252→@gcs_breaker\n   253→@_gcs_retry\n   254→def _gcs_generate_upload_url(\n   255→    filename: str,\n   256→    content_type: str = \"application/pdf\",\n   257→    expiration_minutes: int = 15,\n   258→) -> tuple[str, str]:\n   259→    \"\"\"Generate a signed URL for uploading a file to GCS.\"\"\"\n   260→    blob_path = f\"protocols/{uuid4()}/{filename}\"\n   261→\n   262→    bucket_name = get_bucket_name()\n   263→    client = get_gcs_client()\n   264→    if client is None:\n   265→        raise ValueError(\"GCS client initialization failed. Check GCP credentials.\")\n   266→\n   267→    bucket = client.bucket(bucket_name)\n   268→    blob = bucket.blob(blob_path)\n   269→\n   270→    signed_url = blob.generate_signed_url(\n   271→        version=\"v4\",\n   272→        expiration=timedelta(minutes=expiration_minutes),\n   273→        method=\"PUT\",\n   274→        content_type=content_type,\n   275→    )\n   276→\n   277→    gcs_path = f\"gs://{bucket_name}/{blob_path}\"\n   278→    return (signed_url, gcs_path)\n   279→\n   280→\n   281→@gcs_breaker\n   282→@_gcs_retry\n   283→def _gcs_set_blob_metadata(gcs_path: str, metadata: dict[str, str]) -> None:\n   284→    \"\"\"Set custom metadata on a GCS blob.\"\"\"\n   285→    bucket_name, blob_path = _parse_gcs_uri(gcs_path)\n   286→    client = get_gcs_client()\n   287→    if client is None:\n   288→        raise ValueError(\"GCS client unavailable. Cannot set metadata.\")\n   289→\n   290→    bucket = client.bucket(bucket_name)\n   291→    blob = bucket.blob(blob_path)\n   292→    blob.metadata = metadata\n   293→    blob.patch()\n   294→    logger.info(\"Set metadata on %s: %s\", gcs_path, metadata)\n   295→\n   296→\n   297→@gcs_breaker\n   298→@_gcs_retry\n   299→def _gcs_generate_download_url(gcs_path: str, expiration_minutes: int = 60) -> str:\n   300→    \"\"\"Generate a signed URL for downloading a file from GCS.\"\"\"\n   301→    bucket_name, blob_path = _parse_gcs_uri(gcs_path)\n   302→    client = get_gcs_client()\n   303→    if client is None:\n   304→        raise ValueError(\"GCS client unavailable. ### 2. Models (from `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/shared/src/shared/models.py`)

All SQLModel table models are defined in the shared library. Here are all models with their fields and relationships:

#### **Protocol** (table: `protocol`)

quality_score: float | None
error_reason: str | None
metadata_: Dict[str, Any]  # JSON column

```

### 3. main.py (`/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/main.py`)

**App creation**: `app = FastAPI(lifespan=lifespan)`

**Lifespan**: Creates DB tables, initializes MLflow (if MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI set), starts `OutboxProcessor` as background task with handler for `protocol_uploaded` events.

**Middleware** (in order):
1. `SessionMiddleware` (for OAuth state)
2. `CORSMiddleware` (origins from `CORS_ORIGINS` env var)
3. `MLflowRequestMiddleware` (custom ASGI middleware for request tracing)

**Global exception handler**: Catches unhandled exceptions, returns JSON 500.

**Router mounting**:
```python
# PUBLIC (no auth):
app.include_router(auth_router)                    # prefix=/auth

# PROTECTED (all require get_current_user via Depends):
app.include_router(protocols_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])       # prefix=/protocols
app.include_router(reviews_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])         # prefix=/reviews
app.include_router(entities_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])        # prefix=/entities
app.include_router(search_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])          # prefix=/criteria
app.include_router(terminology_search_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])  # prefix=/api/terminology
app.include_router(integrity_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])       # prefix=/integrity
app.include_router(criterion_rerun_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)]) # prefix=/reviews
app.include_router(batch_compare_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])   # prefix=/reviews
```

**Root endpoints**: `/health`, `/ready` (with DB check), `/`, `/local-upload/{path}`, `/local-files/{path}`

**Pattern**: Auth is applied at the router level via `dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)]` in `include_router()`, NOT on individual endpoints. This means all endpoints in a protected router automatically require auth.

---

### 4. Router Patterns

All routers follow the same structure:

1. **Router creation**: `router = APIRouter(prefix="/...", tags=["..."])`
2. **Pydantic request/response models**: Defined at module top, after imports.
3. **Endpoints**: Use `@router.get/post(path, response_model=...)` with `db: Session = Depends(get_db)` for database access.
4. **Helper functions**: Private `_helper_name()` functions for model conversion and business logic.
5. **No direct `get_current_user` on most endpoints** -- it's applied at the router level in main.py. Exception: `entities.py` uses `current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user)` on individual endpoints as well.

**Existing routers and their endpoints**:

| Router file | Prefix | Endpoints |
|---|---|---|
| `auth.py` | `/auth` | GET `/login`, GET `/callback`, GET `/me`, POST `/dev-login` |
| `protocols.py` | `/protocols` | POST `/upload`, POST `/{id}/confirm-upload`, POST `/{id}/retry`, POST `/{id}/archive`, POST `/{id}/re-extract`, GET `""` (list), GET `/{id}`, GET `/{id}/batches` |
| `reviews.py` | `/reviews` | GET `/batches`, GET `/batches/{id}/metrics`, GET `/batches/{id}/criteria`, POST `/criteria/{id}/action`, GET `/protocols/{id}/pdf-url`, GET `/pending-summary`, GET `/audit-log` |
| `entities.py` | `/entities` | GET `/criteria/{id}`, GET `/batch/{id}`, POST `/{id}/action` |
| `search.py` | `/criteria` | GET `/search` |
| `terminology_search.py` | `/api/terminology` | GET `/{system}/search` |
| `integrity.py` | `/integrity` | GET `/check` |
| `criterion_rerun.py` | `/reviews` | POST `/criteria/{id}/rerun` |
| `batch_compare.py` | `/reviews` | GET `/batch-compare` |

**Pagination pattern** (used by protocols, reviews, search, audit-log):
```python
class SomeListResponse(BaseModel):
    items: list[SomeResponse]
    total: int
    page: int
    page_size: int
    pages: int
```
With query params: `page: int = Query(default=1, ge=1)`, `page_size: int = Query(default=20, ge=1, le=100)`.

---

### 5. Test Fixtures (`/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/tests/conftest.py`)

**`db_engine`** (function scope): Creates in-memory SQLite engine with `StaticPool`. Imports `shared.models` to register tables, then calls `SQLModel.metadata.create_all(engine)`.

**`db_session(db_engine)`** (function scope): Creates a `Session(db_engine)`, yields it, rolls back after each test.

**`test_client(db_session)`** (function scope): Creates `TestClient(app)` with two dependency overrides:
- `get_db` -> yields the test `db_session`
- `get_current_user` -> returns `{"sub": "test-user-id", "email": "test@example.com", "name": "Test User"}`

**`async_client(db_session)`** (function scope): Same overrides but with `httpx.AsyncClient` for async endpoint testing.

**`auth_headers()`**: Returns `{"Authorization": "Bearer <token>"}` with a valid JWT signed with the dev secret.

**`unauthenticated_client(db_session)`** (function scope): Creates `TestClient(app)` with ONLY `get_db` overridden. Does NOT override `get_current_user`, so requests get 401. Used for testing auth enforcement.

All fixtures clear `app.dependency_overrides` after yielding.

**Migration 43_01** (the latest, `43_01_add_unit_value_concept_ids.py`):
- Revision: `43_01_unit_value_concept_ids`
- Down-revision: `42_01_expression_tree`
- Uses `sa.inspect(conn)` to get existing columns, then `batch_alter_table` with conditional `add_column` (idempotent pattern).
- Adds `unit_concept_id` (Integer, nullable) and `value_concept_id` (Integer, nullable) to `atomic_criteria`.

**Common migration patterns**:
- Convention: `{number}_{sub}_{description}.py` for manual migrations, hash-based for autogenerated ones.
- All use `batch_alter_table` for SQLite compatibility (env.py sets `render_as_batch=True`).
- Recent migrations use `sa.inspect(conn)` to check existing columns/tables before adding (idempotent).
- `upgrade()` and `downgrade()` both present.

### 8. The `get_current_user` Dependency

Defined in `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/dependencies.py`:

```python
def get_current_user(authorization: str | None = Header(default=None)) -> dict:
    """Extract and validate JWT from Authorization header.
    Expected format: Bearer <token>
    Returns: Dict with user claims: sub, email, name.
    Raises: HTTPException 401 if token is missing, expired, or invalid.
    """
    if not authorization or not authorization.startswith("Bearer "):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Missing or invalid auth header")

    token = authorization[7:]
    try:
        secret = os.getenv("JWT_SECRET_KEY", "dev-secret-key-change-in-production")
        payload = jwt.decode(token, secret, algorithms=["HS256"])
        return payload
    except jwt.ExpiredSignatureError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Token expired")
    except jwt.InvalidTokenError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token")
```

Returns a `dict` with keys `sub` (user ID), `email`, `name`. It is applied to protected routers at the `include_router()` level in main.py, and also referenced directly via `Depends(get_current_user)` in some endpoints (e.g., in `entities.py` and `search.py`).

Also in `dependencies.py`:
```python
def get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    with Session(engine) as session:
        yield session
```

The `engine` comes from `storage.py` which reads `DATABASE_URL` from environment (required, no fallback) and creates a SQLAlchemy engine.

    78→        )
    79→
    80→    # Start outbox processor as background task
    81→    # Per PIPE-03: criteria_extracted outbox removed. protocol_uploaded retained.
    82→    # protocol_processor.trigger.handle_protocol_uploaded replaces both the
    83→    # old extraction_service.trigger and grounding_service.trigger handlers.
    84→    processor = OutboxProcessor(
    85→        engine=engine,
    86→        handlers={
    87→            "protocol_uploaded": [handle_protocol_uploaded],
    88→        },
    89→    )
    90→    task = asyncio.create_task(processor.start())
    91→    _running_tasks.add(task)
    92→    task.add_done_callback(_running_tasks.discard)
    93→
    94→    yield
    95→
    96→    # Shutdown - stop outbox processor first
    97→    await processor.stop()
    98→
    99→    # Wait for background tasks to complete
   100→    if _running_tasks:
   101→        logger.info(f"Waiting for {len(_running_tasks)} tasks to complete...")
   102→        await asyncio.gather(*_running_tasks, return_exceptions=True)
   103→    logger.info("Shutdown complete")
   104→
   105→
   106→app = FastAPI(lifespan=lifespan)
   107→
   108→# Add SessionMiddleware for OAuth state (must be before CORS)
   109→app.add_middleware(
   110→    SessionMiddleware,
   111→    secret_key=os.getenv("SESSION_SECRET", "dev-session-secret"),
   112→)
   113→
   114→# Configure CORS
   115→# In production, restrict origins to specific domains
   116→cors_origins = os.getenv(
   117→    "CORS_ORIGINS",
   118→    "http://localhost:3000,http://localhost:3001,http://localhost:3002,http://localhost:3003,http://localhost:5173,http://localhost:5174",
   119→).split(",")
   120→
   121→app.add_middleware(
   122→    CORSMiddleware,
   123→    allow_origins=cors_origins,
   124→    allow_credentials=True,
   125→    allow_methods=["*"],
   126→    allow_headers=["*"],
   127→)
   128→
   129→# Add MLflow request tracing middleware
   130→app.add_middleware(MLflowRequestMiddleware)
   131→
   132→
   133→@app.exception_handler(Exception)
   134→async def global_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: Exception) -> JSONResponse:
   135→    """Catch unhandled exceptions and return JSON so CORS headers are preserved."""
   136→    logger.error(
   137→        "Unhandled exception on %s %s: %s", request.method, request.url.path, exc
   138→    )
   139→    return JSONResponse(
   140→        status_code=500,
   141→        content={"detail": "Internal server error"},
   142→    )
   143→
   144→
   145→# Mount auth router (public endpoints - no auth required)
   146→app.include_router(auth_router)
   147→
   148→# Mount protected routers (all endpoints require auth)
   149→app.include_router(protocols_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])
   150→app.include_router(reviews_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])
   151→app.include_router(entities_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])
   152→app.include_router(search_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])
   153→app.include_router(terminology_search_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])
   154→app.include_router(integrity_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])
   155→app.include_router(criterion_rerun_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])
   156→app.include_router(batch_compare_router, dependencies=[Depends(get_current_user)])
   157→
   158→
   159→@app.get("/health")
   160→async def health_check():
   161→    """Liveness probe - is the service running?"""
   162→    return {"status": "healthy"}
   163→
   164→
   165→@app.get("/ready")
   166→async def readiness_check(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
   167→    """Readiness probe - can the service handle requests?"""
   168→    try:
   169→        # Test database connection
   170→        db.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
   171→        return {"status": "ready", "database": "connected"}
   172→    except Exception as e:
   173→        logger.error(f"Readiness check failed: {e}")
   174→        return JSONResponse(
   175→            status_code=503,
   176→            content={
   177→                "status": "not_ready",
   178→                "error": "Database unavailable",
   179→            },
   180→        )
   181→
   182→
   183→@app.get("/")
   184→async def root():
   185→    """Returns a welcome message for the API."""
   186→    return {"message": "Welcome to the API Service"}
   187→
   188→
   189→# --- Local file storage endpoints (dev only) ---
   190→
   191→
   192→@app.put("/local-upload/{blob_path:path}")
   193→async def local_upload(blob_path: str, request: Request):
   194→    """Receive a file upload and store it locally (dev mode)."""
   195→    from api_service.gcs import local_save_file
   196→
   197→    body = await request.body()
   198→    local_save_file(blob_path, body)
   199→    return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content={"status": "ok"})
   200→
   201→
   202→@app.get("/local-files/{blob_path:path}")
   203→async def local_files(blob_path: str):
   204→    """Serve a locally stored file (dev mode)."""
   205→    from api_service.gcs import local_get_file_path
   206→
   207→    file_path = local_get_file_path(blob_path)
   208→    if file_path is None:
   209→        return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"detail": "File not found"})
   210→    return FileResponse(str(file_path), media_type="application/pdf")
   211→

     2→

    11→    JSON,

    25→def _ts_col() -> Column:  # type: ignore[type-arg]
    26→    """Create a created_at timestamp column with server default."""
    27→    return Column(
    28→        DateTime(timezone=True),
    29→        server_default=func.now(),
    30→        nullable=False,
    31→    )
    32→
    33→
    34→def _ts_col_update() -> Column:  # type: ignore[type-arg]

    36→    return Column(

    59→class Protocol(SQLModel, table=True):

    72→

   184→class AtomicCriterion(SQLModel, table=True):
   185→    """Leaf node in a criterion expression tree.
   186→
   187→    Represents a single atomic condition (e.g. "HbA1c >= 7%") decomposed
   188→    from a criterion's field_mappings. Links to the parent Criteria record
   189→    and carries the OMOP concept ID for direct CDM joins.

   194→    __tablename__ = "atomic_criteria"  # type: ignore[assignment]

   200→    criterion_id: str = Field(foreign_key="criteria.id", index=True)
   201→    protocol_id: str = Field(foreign_key="protocol.id", index=True)
   202→    inclusion_exclusion: str = Field()
   203→    entity_concept_id: str | None = Field(default=None, index=True)
   204→    entity_concept_system: str | None = Field(default=None)

   210→    )
   211→    value_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
   212→    unit_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
   213→    unit_concept_id: int | None = Field(
   214→        default=None, sa_column=Column(Integer, nullable=True)
   215→    )
   216→    value_concept_id: int | None = Field(
   217→        default=None, sa_column=Column(Integer, nullable=True)
   218→    )
   219→    negation: bool = Field(default=False, sa_column=Column(Boolean, default=False))
   220→    temporal_constraint: Dict[str, Any] | None = Field(
   221→        default=None, sa_column=Column(JSON)
   222→    )
   223→    original_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
   224→    confidence_score: float | None = Field(default=None)
   225→    human_verified: bool = Field(
   226→        default=False, sa_column=Column(Boolean, default=False)
   227→    )
   228→    human_modified: bool = Field(
   229→        default=False, sa_column=Column(Boolean, default=False)
   230→    )
   231→    created_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col())
   232→    updated_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col_update())
   233→
   234→
   235→class CompositeCriterion(SQLModel, table=True):
   236→    """Branch node in a criterion expression tree.
   237→
   238→    Represents a logical operator (AND/OR/NOT) that combines child nodes.
   239→    Supports nested composition via parent_criterion_id self-reference.
   240→
   241→    Table: composite_criteria
   242→    """
   243→
   244→    __tablename__ = "composite_criteria"  # type: ignore[assignment]
   245→
   246→    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()), primary_key=True)
   247→    criterion_id: str = Field(foreign_key="criteria.id", index=True)
   248→    protocol_id: str = Field(foreign_key="protocol.id", index=True)
   249→    inclusion_exclusion: str = Field()
   250→    logic_operator: str = Field()
   251→    parent_criterion_id: str | None = Field(default=None, index=True)
   252→    original_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
   253→    human_verified: bool = Field(
   254→        default=False, sa_column=Column(Boolean, default=False)
   255→    )
   256→    created_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col())
   257→    updated_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col_update())
   258→
   259→
   260→class CriterionRelationship(SQLModel, table=True):
   261→    """Edge in a criterion expression tree.
   262→
   263→    Links a parent CompositeCriterion to a child (atomic or composite)
   264→    via polymorphic FK. child_type discriminates between the two tables.
   265→    child_sequence preserves operand order.
   266→
   267→    Table: criterion_relationships
   268→    """
   269→
   270→    __tablename__ = "criterion_relationships"  # type: ignore[assignment]
   271→
   272→    parent_criterion_id: str = Field(
   273→        foreign_key="composite_criteria.id", primary_key=True
   274→    )
   275→    child_criterion_id: str = Field(primary_key=True)
   276→    child_type: str = Field()
   277→    child_sequence: int = Field(
   278→        default=0, sa_column=Column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
   279→    )
   280→
   281→
   282→class OutboxEvent(SQLModel, table=True):
   283→    """Transactional outbox for event publishing."""
   284→
   285→    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()), primary_key=True)
   286→    event_type: str = Field(index=True)
   287→    aggregate_type: str = Field()
   288→    aggregate_id: str = Field(index=True)
   289→    payload: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict, sa_column=Column(JSON))
   290→    idempotency_key: str = Field(sa_column=Column(String, unique=True, nullable=False))
   291→    status: str = Field(default="pending", index=True)
   292→    retry_count: int = Field(default=0)
   293→    published_at: datetime | None = Field(default=None)
   294→    created_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col())
   295→
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/exporters/__init__.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Shared data loader for protocol export builders.\n\nLoads all structured criteria data for a protocol into a single\ndataclass consumed by the CIRCE, FHIR Group, and evaluation SQL builders.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n\nfrom sqlmodel import Session, select\nfrom shared.models import (\n    AtomicCriterion,\n    CompositeCriterion,\n    Criteria,\n    CriteriaBatch,\n    CriterionRelationship,\n    Protocol,\n)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ProtocolExportData:\n    \"\"\"All structured criteria data needed by export builders.\"\"\"\n\n    protocol: Protocol\n    criteria: list[Criteria]\n    atomics: list[AtomicCriterion]\n    composites: list[CompositeCriterion]\n    relationships: list[CriterionRelationship]\n    atomics_by_id: dict[str, AtomicCriterion] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    composites_by_id: dict[str, CompositeCriterion] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    children_by_parent: dict[str, list[CriterionRelationship]] = field(\n        default_factory=dict\n    )\n    criteria_by_id: dict[str, Criteria] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\ndef load_protocol_export_data(\n    db: Session, protocol_id: str\n) -> ProtocolExportData | None:\n    \"\"\"Load all export data for a protocol.\n\n    Queries the active (non-archived) batch and all associated\n    criteria, atomic criteria, composite criteria, and relationships.\n\n    Returns:\n        ProtocolExportData if the protocol exists and has structured\n        criteria, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    protocol = db.get(Protocol, protocol_id)\n    if protocol is None:\n        return None\n\n    # Find the active (non-archived) batch\n    batch = db.exec(\n        select(CriteriaBatch)\n        .where(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id == protocol_id)\n        .where(CriteriaBatch.is_archived == False)  # noqa: E712\n        .order_by(CriteriaBatch.created_at.desc())  # type: ignore[union-attr]\n    ).first()\n    if batch is None:\n        return None\n\n    # Load criteria from active batch\n    criteria = list(\n        db.exec(select(Criteria).where(Criteria.batch_id == batch.id)).all()\n    )\n    if not criteria:\n        return None\n\n    # Load atomics and composites for this protocol\n    atomics = list(\n        db.exec(\n            select(AtomicCriterion).where(\n                AtomicCriterion.protocol_id == protocol_id\n            )\n        ).all()\n    )\n    composites = list(\n        db.exec(\n            select(CompositeCriterion).where(\n                CompositeCriterion.protocol_id == protocol_id\n            )\n        ).all()\n    )\n\n    # Load relationships for all composites\n    composite_ids = [c.id for c in composites]\n    relationships: list[CriterionRelationship] = []\n    if composite_ids:\n        relationships = list(\n            db.exec(\n                select(CriterionRelationship).where(\n                    CriterionRelationship.parent_criterion_id.in_(  # type: ignore[union-attr]\n                        composite_ids\n                    )\n                )\n            ).all()\n        )\n\n    # Build lookup dicts\n    atomics_by_id = {a.id: a for a in atomics}\n    composites_by_id = {c.id: c for c in composites}\n    criteria_by_id = {c.id: c for c in criteria}\n\n    children_by_parent: dict[str, list[CriterionRelationship]] = {}\n    for rel in relationships:\n        children_by_parent.setdefault(rel.parent_criterion_id, []).append(rel)\n    # Sort children by child_sequence\n    for children in children_by_parent.values():\n        children.sort(key=lambda r: r.child_sequence)\n\n    return ProtocolExportData(\n        protocol=protocol,\n        criteria=criteria,\n        atomics=atomics,\n        composites=composites,\n        relationships=relationships,\n        atomics_by_id=atomics_by_id,\n        composites_by_id=composites_by_id,\n        children_by_parent=children_by_parent,\n        criteria_by_id=criteria_by_id,\n    )\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/exporters/circe_builder.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"OHDSI CIRCE CohortExpression JSON builder.\n\nConverts structured criteria (expression trees with atomic/composite nodes)\ninto the CIRCE JSON format consumed by OHDSI Atlas and WebAPI.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom api_service.exporters import ProtocolExportData\nfrom shared.models import AtomicCriterion, CriterionRelationship\n\n# Map our entity_domain values to CIRCE domain table names\n_DOMAIN_TO_CIRCE: dict[str, str] = {\n    \"condition\": \"ConditionOccurrence\",\n    \"measurement\": \"Measurement\",\n    \"drug\": \"DrugExposure\",\n    \"procedure\": \"ProcedureOccurrence\",\n    \"observation\": \"Observation\",\n    \"device\": \"DeviceExposure\",\n    \"visit\": \"VisitOccurrence\",\n}\n\n# Map relation_operator to CIRCE Op string\n_OP_MAP: dict[str, str] = {\n    \">\": \"gt\",\n    \">=\": \"gte\",\n    \"<\": \"lt\",\n    \"<=\": \"lte\",\n    \"=\": \"eq\",\n    \"==\": \"eq\",\n    \"!=\": \"neq\",\n    \"gt\": \"gt\",\n    \"gte\": \"gte\",\n    \"lt\": \"lt\",\n    \"lte\": \"lte\",\n    \"eq\": \"eq\",\n    \"neq\": \"neq\",\n}\n\n\ndef build_circe_export(data: ProtocolExportData) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Build a CIRCE CohortExpression JSON from structured criteria.\n\n    Args:\n        data: Loaded protocol export data.\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict representing the CIRCE CohortExpression.\n    \"\"\"\n    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    concept_set_index: dict[str, int] = {}  # concept_id -> index in concept_sets\n\n    inclusion_criteria: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    censoring_criteria: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n\n    for criterion in data.criteria:\n        tree = criterion.structured_criterion\n        if not tree:\n            continue\n\n        is_exclusion = criterion.criteria_type == \"exclusion\"\n        group = _build_criteria_group(\n            tree, data, concept_sets, concept_set_index\n        )\n        if group is None:\n            continue\n\n        if is_exclusion:\n            censoring_criteria.append(group)\n        else:\n            inclusion_criteria.append(group)\n\n    expression: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"ConceptSets\": concept_sets,\n        \"PrimaryCriteria\": {\n            \"CriteriaList\": [],\n            \"ObservationWindow\": {\n                \"PriorDays\": 0,\n                \"PostDays\": 0,\n            },\n            \"PrimaryCriteriaLimit\": {\"Type\": \"First\"},\n        },\n        \"AdditionalCriteria\": {\n            \"Type\": \"ALL\",\n            \"CriteriaList\": inclusion_criteria,\n        },\n        \"CensoringCriteria\": censoring_criteria,\n    }\n\n    return expression\n\n\ndef _get_concept_id(atomic: AtomicCriterion) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Get the best concept ID for an atomic criterion.\n\n    Prefers omop_concept_id, falls back to entity_concept_id.\n    Returns None if neither is a valid integer.\n    \"\"\"\n    for field_val in (atomic.omop_concept_id, atomic.entity_concept_id):\n        if field_val is not None:\n            try:\n                return int(field_val)\n            except (ValueError, TypeError):\n                continue\n    return None\n\n\ndef _ensure_concept_set(\n    atomic: AtomicCriterion,\n    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    concept_set_index: dict[str, int],\n) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Ensure a ConceptSet exists for the atomic's concept ID.\n\n    Returns the ConceptSet index, or None if no valid concept ID.\n    \"\"\"\n    concept_id = _get_concept_id(atomic)\n    if concept_id is None:\n        return None\n\n    key = str(concept_id)\n    if key in concept_set_index:\n        return concept_set_index[key]\n\n    idx = len(concept_sets)\n    concept_sets.append({\n        \"id\": idx,\n        \"name\": atomic.original_text or f\"Concept {concept_id}\",\n        \"expression\": {\n            \"items\": [\n                {\n                    \"concept\": {\n                        \"CONCEPT_ID\": concept_id,\n                        \"CONCEPT_NAME\": atomic.original_text or \"\",\n                    },\n                    \"isExcluded\": False,\n                    \"includeDescendants\": True,\n                    \"includeMapped\": False,\n                }\n            ],\n        },\n    })\n    concept_set_index[key] = idx\n    return idx\n\n\ndef _build_atomic_criteria(\n    atomic: AtomicCriterion,\n    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    concept_set_index: dict[str, int],\n) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Build a CIRCE criteria entry from an AtomicCriterion.\"\"\"\n    cs_idx = _ensure_concept_set(atomic, concept_sets, concept_set_index)\n    if cs_idx is None:\n        return None\n\n    domain = _DOMAIN_TO_CIRCE.get(\n        (atomic.entity_domain or \"\").lower(), \"ConditionOccurrence\"\n    )\n\n    criteria_entry: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"Criteria\": {\n            domain: {\n                \"CodesetId\": cs_idx,\n            },\n        },\n    }\n\n    # Handle negation: absence = OccurrenceCount of 0\n    if atomic.negation:\n        criteria_entry[\"Criteria\"][\"OccurrenceCount\"] = {\n            \"Value\": 0,\n            \"Op\": \"eq\",\n        }\n\n    # Add value filter for measurements\n    if atomic.relation_operator and atomic.value_numeric is not None:\n        op_str = _OP_MAP.get(atomic.relation_operator, \"eq\")\n        value_filter: dict[str, Any] = {\n            \"Value\": atomic.value_numeric,\n            \"Op\": op_str,\n        }\n        if atomic.unit_concept_id is not None:\n            value_filter[\"UnitConceptId\"] = atomic.unit_concept_id\n        criteria_entry[\"Criteria\"][\"ValueAsNumber\"] = value_filter\n\n    return criteria_entry\n\n\ndef _build_criteria_group(\n    tree: dict[str, Any],\n    data: ProtocolExportData,\n    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    concept_set_index: dict[str, int],\n) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Recursively build a CIRCE CriteriaGroup from an expression tree node.\n\n    The tree uses ExpressionNode format:\n    - type: AND/OR/NOT/ATOMIC\n    - atomic_criterion_id: FK for ATOMIC nodes\n    - children: list of child nodes for AND/OR/NOT\n    \"\"\"\n    node_type = tree.get(\"type\", \"\").upper()\n\n    if node_type == \"ATOMIC\":\n        atomic_id = tree.get(\"atomic_criterion_id\")\n        if not atomic_id:\n            return None\n        atomic = data.atomics_by_id.get(atomic_id)\n        if not atomic:\n            return None\n        return _build_atomic_criteria(atomic, concept_sets, concept_set_index)\n\n    if node_type in (\"AND\", \"OR\"):\n        children = tree.get(\"children\", [])\n        child_criteria = []\n        for child in children:\n            result = _build_criteria_group(\n                child, data, concept_sets, concept_set_index\n            )\n            if result is not None:\n                child_criteria.append(result)\n\n        if not child_criteria:\n            return None\n\n        group_type = \"ALL\" if node_type == \"AND\" else \"ANY\"\n        return {\n            \"Type\": group_type,\n            \"CriteriaList\": child_criteria,\n        }\n\n    if node_type == \"NOT\":\n        children = tree.get(\"children\", [])\n        if not children:\n            return None\n        inner = _build_criteria_group(\n            children[0], data, concept_sets, concept_set_index\n        )\n        if inner is None:\n            return None\n        # Wrap in negated occurrence\n        if \"Criteria\" in inner:\n            inner[\"Criteria\"][\"OccurrenceCount\"] = {\n                \"Value\": 0,\n                \"Op\": \"eq\",\n            }\n        return inner\n\n    return None\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/exporters/fhir_group_builder.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"FHIR R4 Group resource builder (EBM IG).\n\nConverts structured criteria into a FHIR Group resource with\ncharacteristics representing eligibility criteria.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any\nfrom uuid import uuid4\n\nfrom api_service.exporters import ProtocolExportData\nfrom shared.models import AtomicCriterion\n\n# Map entity_concept_system to FHIR system URIs\n_SYSTEM_URI_MAP: dict[str, str] = {\n    \"snomed\": \"http://snomed.info/sct\",\n    \"snomedct\": \"http://snomed.info/sct\",\n    \"loinc\": \"http://loinc.org\",\n    \"rxnorm\": \"http://www.nlm.nih.gov/research/umls/rxnorm\",\n    \"icd10\": \"http://hl7.org/fhir/sid/icd-10\",\n    \"icd10cm\": \"http://hl7.org/fhir/sid/icd-10-cm\",\n    \"cpt\": \"http://www.ama-assn.org/go/cpt\",\n    \"hcpcs\": \"urn:oid:2.16.840.1.113883.6.285\",\n    \"omop\": \"http://ohdsi.org/omop/concept\",\n}\n\n# Map our relation_operator to FHIR comparator\n_COMPARATOR_MAP: dict[str, str] = {\n    \">\": \"gt\",\n    \">=\": \"ge\",\n    \"<\": \"lt\",\n    \"<=\": \"le\",\n    \"gt\": \"gt\",\n    \"gte\": \"ge\",\n    \"lt\": \"lt\",\n    \"lte\": \"le\",\n}\n\n\ndef build_fhir_group_export(data: ProtocolExportData) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Build a FHIR R4 Group resource from structured criteria.\n\n    Args:\n        data: Loaded protocol export data.\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict representing the FHIR Group resource.\n    \"\"\"\n    characteristics: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n\n    for criterion in data.criteria:\n        tree = criterion.structured_criterion\n        if not tree:\n            continue\n\n        is_exclusion = criterion.criteria_type == \"exclusion\"\n        chars = _build_characteristics_from_tree(tree, data, exclude=is_exclusion)\n        characteristics.extend(chars)\n\n    group: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"resourceType\": \"Group\",\n        \"id\": data.protocol.id,\n        \"type\": \"person\",\n        \"actual\": False,\n        \"name\": data.protocol.title,\n        \"characteristic\": characteristics,\n    }\n\n    # Set combination method if we have characteristics\n    if characteristics:\n        group[\"extension\"] = [\n            {\n                \"url\": (\n                    \"http://hl7.org/fhir/uv/ebm/StructureDefinition\"\n                    \"/characteristic-combination\"\n                ),\n                \"valueCode\": \"all-of\",\n            }\n        ]\n\n    return group\n\n\ndef _build_characteristics_from_tree(\n    tree: dict[str, Any],\n    data: ProtocolExportData,\n    exclude: bool = False,\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Recursively build FHIR characteristics from an expression tree node.\"\"\"\n    node_type = tree.get(\"type\", \"\").upper()\n\n    if node_type == \"ATOMIC\":\n        atomic_id = tree.get(\"atomic_criterion_id\")\n        if not atomic_id:\n            return []\n        atomic = data.atomics_by_id.get(atomic_id)\n        if not atomic:\n            return []\n        char = _build_characteristic(atomic, exclude=exclude)\n        return [char] if char else []\n\n    if node_type == \"AND\":\n        children = tree.get(\"children\", [])\n        result = []\n        for child in children:\n            result.extend(\n                _build_characteristics_from_tree(child, data, exclude=exclude)\n            )\n        return result\n\n    if node_type == \"OR\":\n        children = tree.get(\"children\", [])\n        # OR requires a nested contained Group with any-of combination\n        or_chars: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        for child in children:\n            or_chars.extend(\n                _build_characteristics_from_tree(child, data, exclude=exclude)\n            )\n        if not or_chars:\n            return []\n\n        nested_group_id = str(uuid4())\n        nested_group: dict[str, Any] = {\n            \"resourceType\": \"Group\",\n            \"id\": nested_group_id,\n            \"type\": \"person\",\n            \"actual\": False,\n            \"characteristic\": or_chars,\n            \"extension\": [\n                {\n                    \"url\": (\n                        \"http://hl7.org/fhir/uv/ebm/StructureDefinition\"\n                        \"/characteristic-combination\"\n                    ),\n                    \"valueCode\": \"any-of\",\n                }\n            ],\n        }\n        return [\n            {\n                \"code\": {\n                    \"text\": \"Nested OR group\",\n                },\n                \"valueReference\": {\n                    \"reference\": f\"#/{nested_group_id}\",\n                },\n                \"exclude\": exclude,\n                \"_contained\": nested_group,\n            }\n        ]\n\n    if node_type == \"NOT\":\n        children = tree.get(\"children\", [])\n        if not children:\n            return []\n        # Invert the exclude flag\n        return _build_characteristics_from_tree(\n            children[0], data, exclude=not exclude\n        )\n\n    return []\n\n\ndef _get_system_uri(system: str | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Map an entity_concept_system to a FHIR system URI.\"\"\"\n    if not system:\n        return \"http://ohdsi.org/omop/concept\"\n    return _SYSTEM_URI_MAP.get(system.lower(), f\"urn:oid:{system}\")\n\n\ndef _get_concept_code(atomic: AtomicCriterion) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Get the best concept code for an atomic criterion.\"\"\"\n    for val in (atomic.omop_concept_id, atomic.entity_concept_id):\n        if val is not None:\n            return str(val)\n    return None\n\n\ndef _build_characteristic(\n    atomic: AtomicCriterion,\n    exclude: bool = False,\n) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Build a single FHIR Group characteristic from an AtomicCriterion.\"\"\"\n    concept_code = _get_concept_code(atomic)\n    if concept_code is None:\n        return None\n\n    system_uri = _get_system_uri(atomic.entity_concept_system)\n\n    char: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"code\": {\n            \"coding\": [\n                {\n                    \"system\": system_uri,\n                    \"code\": concept_code,\n                    \"display\": atomic.original_text or \"\",\n                }\n            ],\n        },\n        \"exclude\": exclude or atomic.negation,\n    }\n\n    # Add value: quantity or text\n    if atomic.value_numeric is not None:\n        quantity: dict[str, Any] = {\n            \"value\": atomic.value_numeric,\n        }\n        if atomic.unit_text:\n            quantity[\"unit\"] = atomic.unit_text\n        if atomic.relation_operator:\n            comparator = _COMPARATOR_MAP.get(atomic.relation_operator)\n            if comparator:\n                quantity[\"comparator\"] = comparator\n        char[\"valueQuantity\"] = quantity\n    elif atomic.value_text:\n        char[\"valueCodeableConcept\"] = {\n            \"text\": atomic.value_text,\n        }\n    else:\n        # Boolean presence/absence\n        char[\"valueBoolean\"] = not atomic.negation\n\n    return char\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/exporters/evaluation_sql_builder.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"OMOP CDM v5.4 evaluation SQL generator.\n\nGenerates ready-to-run SQL that evaluates a protocol's eligibility criteria\nagainst an OMOP CDM database. The SQL is returned as text, not executed.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom api_service.exporters import ProtocolExportData\nfrom shared.models import AtomicCriterion\n\n# Map entity_domain to OMOP CDM table + concept_id column\n_DOMAIN_TABLE: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] = {\n    \"condition\": (\"condition_occurrence\", \"condition_concept_id\"),\n    \"measurement\": (\"measurement\", \"measurement_concept_id\"),\n    \"drug\": (\"drug_exposure\", \"drug_concept_id\"),\n    \"procedure\": (\"procedure_occurrence\", \"procedure_concept_id\"),\n    \"observation\": (\"observation\", \"observation_concept_id\"),\n    \"device\": (\"device_exposure\", \"device_concept_id\"),\n    \"visit\": (\"visit_occurrence\", \"visit_concept_id\"),\n}\n\n# Map relation_operator to SQL operator\n_SQL_OP: dict[str, str] = {\n    \">\": \">\",\n    \">=\": \">=\",\n    \"<\": \"<\",\n    \"<=\": \"<=\",\n    \"=\": \"=\",\n    \"==\": \"=\",\n    \"!=\": \"!=\",\n    \"gt\": \">\",\n    \"gte\": \">=\",\n    \"lt\": \"<\",\n    \"lte\": \"<=\",\n    \"eq\": \"=\",\n    \"neq\": \"!=\",\n}\n\n\ndef build_evaluation_sql(data: ProtocolExportData) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Generate OMOP CDM evaluation SQL from structured criteria.\n\n    Produces:\n    1. Per-atomic CTEs using concept_ancestor for descendant expansion\n    2. Domain-specific evaluation logic\n    3. Final eligibility query combining inclusion/exclusion\n\n    Args:\n        data: Loaded protocol export data.\n\n    Returns:\n        SQL string ready for execution against an OMOP CDM v5.4 database.\n    \"\"\"\n    ctes: list[str] = []\n    inclusion_cte_names: list[str] = []\n    exclusion_cte_names: list[str] = []\n\n    for i, atomic in enumerate(data.atomics):\n        concept_id = _get_valid_concept_id(atomic)\n        if concept_id is None:\n            continue\n\n        cte_name = f\"cte_{i}\"\n        cte_sql = _build_atomic_cte(atomic, concept_id, cte_name)\n        ctes.append(cte_sql)\n\n        if atomic.inclusion_exclusion == \"exclusion\" or atomic.negation:\n            exclusion_cte_names.append(cte_name)\n        else:\n            inclusion_cte_names.append(cte_name)\n\n    if not ctes:\n        return \"-- No structured criteria with valid concept IDs found.\\nSELECT NULL AS person_id WHERE 1=0;\\n\"\n\n    parts: list[str] = []\n    parts.append(\"-- Auto-generated OMOP CDM v5.4 eligibility evaluation SQL\")\n    parts.append(\n        f\"-- Protocol: {data.protocol.title} ({data.protocol.id})\"\n    )\n    parts.append(f\"-- Generated from {len(ctes)} atomic criteria\\n\")\n    parts.append(\"WITH\")\n    parts.append(\",\\n\\n\".join(ctes))\n\n    # Build final SELECT\n    parts.append(\"\\n\\nSELECT p.person_id\")\n    parts.append(\"FROM person p\")\n\n    # Inclusion: require all\n    for cte_name in inclusion_cte_names:\n        parts.append(\n            f\"WHERE EXISTS (\\n\"\n            f\"    SELECT 1 FROM {cte_name} c\\n\"\n            f\"    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id\\n\"\n            f\")\"\n            if cte_name == inclusion_cte_names[0]\n            else f\"AND EXISTS (\\n\"\n            f\"    SELECT 1 FROM {cte_name} c\\n\"\n            f\"    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id\\n\"\n            f\")\"\n        )\n\n    # Exclusion: reject any\n    for cte_name in exclusion_cte_names:\n        prefix = \"AND\" if inclusion_cte_names else (\n            \"WHERE\" if cte_name == exclusion_cte_names[0] else \"AND\"\n        )\n        parts.append(\n            f\"{prefix} NOT EXISTS (\\n\"\n            f\"    SELECT 1 FROM {cte_name} c\\n\"\n            f\"    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id\\n\"\n            f\")\"\n        )\n\n    parts.append(\";\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(parts)\n\n\ndef _get_valid_concept_id(atomic: AtomicCriterion) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Get a validated integer concept ID for SQL interpolation.\"\"\"\n    for val in (atomic.omop_concept_id, atomic.entity_concept_id):\n        if val is not None:\n            try:\n                return int(val)\n            except (ValueError, TypeError):\n                continue\n    return None\n\n\ndef _build_atomic_cte(\n    atomic: AtomicCriterion, concept_id: int, cte_name: str\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build a CTE for a single atomic criterion.\"\"\"\n    domain = (atomic.entity_domain or \"condition\").lower()\n\n    # Demographics: special handling for age\n    if domain == \"demographics\":\n        return _build_demographics_cte(atomic, cte_name)\n\n    table_name, concept_col = _DOMAIN_TABLE.get(\n        domain, (\"condition_occurrence\", \"condition_concept_id\")\n    )\n\n    lines: list[str] = []\n    lines.append(f\"{cte_name} AS (\")\n    lines.append(f\"    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id\")\n    lines.append(f\"    FROM {table_name} t\")\n    lines.append(f\"    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca\")\n    lines.append(f\"        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.{concept_col}\")\n    lines.append(f\"    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = {concept_id}\")\n\n    # Measurement value filter\n    if domain == \"measurement\" and atomic.relation_operator and atomic.value_numeric is not None:\n        sql_op = _SQL_OP.get(atomic.relation_operator, \"=\")\n        lines.append(\n            f\"    AND t.value_as_number {sql_op} {atomic.value_numeric}\"\n        )\n        if atomic.unit_concept_id is not None:\n            lines.append(\n                f\"    AND t.unit_concept_id = {atomic.unit_concept_id}\"\n            )\n\n    lines.append(\")\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\ndef _build_demographics_cte(\n    atomic: AtomicCriterion, cte_name: str\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build a CTE for demographics-based criteria (age).\"\"\"\n    lines: list[str] = []\n    lines.append(f\"{cte_name} AS (\")\n    lines.append(f\"    SELECT p.person_id\")\n    lines.append(f\"    FROM person p\")\n\n    if atomic.relation_operator and atomic.value_numeric is not None:\n        sql_op = _SQL_OP.get(atomic.relation_operator, \"=\")\n        lines.append(\n            f\"    WHERE EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE) - p.year_of_birth\"\n            f\" {sql_op} {int(atomic.value_numeric)}\"\n        )\n    else:\n        lines.append(\"    WHERE 1=1\")\n\n    lines.append(\")\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/exports.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Export endpoints for protocol criteria in standard formats.\n\nProvides CIRCE (OHDSI), FHIR Group, and evaluation SQL exports\nfor protocols that have completed structured criteria processing.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException\nfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\nfrom sqlmodel import Session\n\nfrom api_service.dependencies import get_db\nfrom api_service.exporters import load_protocol_export_data\nfrom api_service.exporters.circe_builder import build_circe_export\nfrom api_service.exporters.evaluation_sql_builder import build_evaluation_sql\nfrom api_service.exporters.fhir_group_builder import build_fhir_group_export\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/protocols\", tags=[\"exports\"])\n\n\nclass ExportStats(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Summary statistics for an export.\"\"\"\n\n    criteria_count: int\n    atomic_count: int\n    composite_count: int\n    relationship_count: int\n\n\nclass CirceExportResponse(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"CIRCE CohortExpression export response.\"\"\"\n\n    expression: dict[str, Any]\n    stats: ExportStats\n\n\nclass FhirGroupExportResponse(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"FHIR Group resource export response.\"\"\"\n\n    resource: dict[str, Any]\n    stats: ExportStats\n\n\ndef _get_export_data_or_404(db: Session, protocol_id: str):\n    \"\"\"Load export data or raise 404.\"\"\"\n    data = load_protocol_export_data(db, protocol_id)\n    if data is None:\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=404,\n            detail=f\"Protocol {protocol_id} not found or has no structured criteria\",\n        )\n    return data\n\n\ndef _make_stats(data) -> ExportStats:\n    \"\"\"Build export stats from loaded data.\"\"\"\n    return ExportStats(\n        criteria_count=len(data.criteria),\n        atomic_count=len(data.atomics),\n        composite_count=len(data.composites),\n        relationship_count=len(data.relationships),\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/{protocol_id}/export/circe\",\n    response_model=CirceExportResponse,\n)\ndef export_circe(\n    protocol_id: str,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n) -> CirceExportResponse:\n    \"\"\"Export protocol criteria as OHDSI CIRCE CohortExpression JSON.\"\"\"\n    data = _get_export_data_or_404(db, protocol_id)\n    expression = build_circe_export(data)\n    return CirceExportResponse(\n        expression=expression,\n        stats=_make_stats(data),\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/{protocol_id}/export/fhir-group\",\n    response_model=FhirGroupExportResponse,\n)\ndef export_fhir_group(\n    protocol_id: str,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n) -> FhirGroupExportResponse:\n    \"\"\"Export protocol criteria as FHIR R4 Group resource.\"\"\"\n    data = _get_export_data_or_404(db, protocol_id)\n    resource = build_fhir_group_export(data)\n    return FhirGroupExportResponse(\n        resource=resource,\n        stats=_make_stats(data),\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/{protocol_id}/export/evaluation-sql\",\n    response_class=PlainTextResponse,\n)\ndef export_evaluation_sql(\n    protocol_id: str,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n) -> PlainTextResponse:\n    \"\"\"Export protocol criteria as OMOP CDM evaluation SQL.\"\"\"\n    data = _get_export_data_or_404(db, protocol_id)\n    sql = build_evaluation_sql(data)\n    return PlainTextResponse(content=sql, media_type=\"text/plain\")\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/alembic/versions/44_01_add_export_indexes.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Add indexes to optimize export join patterns.\n\nPhase 4: Indexes for CIRCE, FHIR Group, and evaluation SQL exports.\n\nRevision ID: 44_01_export_indexes\nRevises: 43_01_unit_value_concept_ids\nCreate Date: 2026-02-19 00:00:00.000000\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Sequence, Union\n\nimport sqlalchemy as sa\n\nfrom alembic import op\n\n# revision identifiers, used by Alembic.\nrevision: str = \"44_01_export_indexes\"\ndown_revision: Union[str, None] = \"43_01_unit_value_concept_ids\"\nbranch_labels: Union[str, Sequence[str], None] = None\ndepends_on: Union[str, Sequence[str], None] = None\n\n# Index definitions: (index_name, table_name, columns)\n_INDEXES = [\n    (\n        \"ix_atomic_domain_concept\",\n        \"atomic_criteria\",\n        [\"entity_domain\", \"omop_concept_id\"],\n    ),\n    (\n        \"ix_composite_proto_incl\",\n        \"composite_criteria\",\n        [\"protocol_id\", \"inclusion_exclusion\"],\n    ),\n    (\n        \"ix_rel_child_criterion\",\n        \"criterion_relationships\",\n        [\"child_criterion_id\"],\n    ),\n    (\n        \"ix_atomic_crit_incl\",\n        \"atomic_criteria\",\n        [\"criterion_id\", \"inclusion_exclusion\"],\n    ),\n]\n\n\ndef upgrade() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Add export-optimized indexes (idempotent).\"\"\"\n    conn = op.get_bind()\n    inspector = sa.inspect(conn)\n\n    for index_name, table_name, columns in _INDEXES:\n        existing = {idx[\"name\"] for idx in inspector.get_indexes(table_name)}\n        if index_name not in existing:\n            op.create_index(index_name, table_name, columns)\n\n\ndef downgrade() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Remove export-optimized indexes.\"\"\"\n    for index_name, table_name, _columns in reversed(_INDEXES):\n        op.drop_index(index_name, table_name=table_name)\n"
}

     1→"""Integration tests for protocol API endpoints (upload, confirm, list, detail)."""
     2→
     3→import base64
     4→from unittest.mock import patch
     5→
     6→from shared.models import Protocol

exists but batch has no criteria -> 404.\"\"\"\n        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)\n        _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)\n        db_session.commit()\n        r = test_client.get(f\"/protocols/{proto.id}/export/circe\")\n        assert r.status_code == 404\n\n    def test_stats_match_data(self, test_client, db_session):\n        \"\"\"Response stats should reflect actual data counts.\"\"\"\n        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)\n        batch = _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)\n\n        a1_id = str(uuid4())\n        a2_id = str(uuid4())\n        tree = {\n            \"type\": \"AND\",\n            \"children\": [\n                {\"type\": \"ATOMIC\", \"atomic_criterion_id\": a1_id},\n                {\"type\": \"ATOMIC\", \"atomic_criterion_id\": a2_id},\n            ],\n        }\n        crit = _make_criterion(\n            db_session, batch.id, structured_criterion=tree\n        )\n        comp = _make_composite(db_session, crit.id, proto.id)\n        _make_atomic(db_session, crit.id, proto.id, id=a1_id)\n        _make_atomic(db_session, crit.id, proto.id, id=a2_id,\n                      omop_concept_id=\"201826\", entity_domain=\"condition\")\n        _make_relationship(db_session, comp.id, a1_id, \"atomic\", 0)\n        _make_relationship(db_session, comp.id, a2_id, \"atomic\", 1)\n        db_session.commit()\n\n        r = test_client.get(f\"/protocols/{proto.id}/export/circe\")\n        assert r.status_code == 200\n        stats = r.json()[\"stats\"]\n        assert stats[\"criteria_count\"] == 1\n        assert stats[\"atomic_count\"] == 2\n        assert stats[\"composite_count\"] == 1\n        assert stats[\"relationship_count\"] == 2\n\n    def test_auth_required(self, unauthenticated_client, db_session):\n        \"\"\"Export endpoints require authentication.\"\"\"\n        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)\n        db_session.commit()\n ```
     1→"""OMOP CDM v5.4 evaluation SQL generator.
     2→
     3→Generates ready-to-run SQL that evaluates a protocol's eligibility criteria
     4→against an OMOP CDM database. The SQL is returned as text, not executed.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion
    10→
    11→from api_service.exporters import ProtocolExportData
    12→
    13→# Map entity_domain to OMOP CDM table + concept_id column
    14→_DOMAIN_TABLE: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] = {
    15→    "condition": ("condition_occurrence", "condition_concept_id"),
    16→    "measurement": ("measurement", "measurement_concept_id"),
    17→    "drug": ("drug_exposure", "drug_concept_id"),
    18→    "procedure": ("procedure_occurrence", "procedure_concept_id"),
    19→    "observation": ("observation", "observation_concept_id"),
    20→    "device": ("device_exposure", "device_concept_id"),
    21→    "visit": ("visit_occurrence", "visit_concept_id"),
    22→}
    23→
    24→# Map relation_operator to SQL operator
    25→_SQL_OP: dict[str, str] = {
    26→    ">": ">",
    27→    ">=": ">=",
    28→    "<": "<",
    29→    "<=": "<=",
    30→    "=": "=",
    31→    "==": "=",
    32→    "!=": "!=",
    33→    "gt": ">",
    34→    "gte": ">=",
    35→    "lt": "<",
    36→    "lte": "<=",
    37→    "eq": "=",
    38→    "neq": "!=",
    39→}
    40→
    41→
    42→def build_evaluation_sql(data: ProtocolExportData) -> str:
    43→    """Generate OMOP CDM evaluation SQL from structured criteria.
    44→
    45→    Produces:
    46→    1. Per-atomic CTEs using concept_ancestor for descendant expansion
    47→    2. Domain-specific evaluation logic
    48→    3. Final eligibility query combining inclusion/exclusion
    49→
    50→    Args:
    51→        data: Loaded protocol export data.
    52→
    53→    Returns:
    54→        SQL string ready for execution against an OMOP CDM v5.4 database.
    55→    """
    56→    ctes: list[str] = []
    57→    inclusion_cte_names: list[str] = []
    58→    exclusion_cte_names: list[str] = []
    59→
    60→    for i, atomic in enumerate(data.atomics):
    61→        concept_id = _get_valid_concept_id(atomic)
    62→        if concept_id is None:
    63→            continue
    64→
    65→        cte_name = f"cte_{i}"
    66→        cte_sql = _build_atomic_cte(atomic, concept_id, cte_name)
    67→        ctes.append(cte_sql)
    68→
    69→        if atomic.inclusion_exclusion == "exclusion" or atomic.negation:
    70→            exclusion_cte_names.append(cte_name)
    71→        else:
    72→            inclusion_cte_names.append(cte_name)
    73→
    74→    if not ctes:
    75→        return "-- No structured criteria with valid concept IDs found.\nSELECT NULL AS person_id WHERE 1=0;\n"
    76→
    77→    parts: list[str] = []
    78→    parts.append("-- Auto-generated OMOP CDM v5.4 eligibility evaluation SQL")
    79→    parts.append(
    80→        f"-- Protocol: {data.protocol.title} ({data.protocol.id})"
    81→    )
    82→    parts.append(f"-- Generated from {len(ctes)} atomic criteria\n")
    83→    parts.append("WITH")
    84→    parts.append(",\n\n".join(ctes))
    85→
    86→    # Build final SELECT
    87→    parts.append("\n\nSELECT p.person_id")
    88→    parts.append("FROM person p")
    89→
    90→    # Inclusion: require all
    91→    for cte_name in inclusion_cte_names:
    92→        parts.append(
    93→            f"WHERE EXISTS (\n"
    94→            f"    SELECT 1 FROM {cte_name} c\n"
    95→            f"    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id\n"
    96→            f")"
    97→            if cte_name == inclusion_cte_names[0]
    98→            else f"AND EXISTS (\n"
    99→            f"    SELECT 1 FROM {cte_name} c\n"
   100→            f"    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id\n"
   101→            f")"
   102→        )
   103→
   104→    # Exclusion: reject any
   105→    for cte_name in exclusion_cte_names:
   106→        prefix = "AND" if inclusion_cte_names else (
   107→            "WHERE" if cte_name == exclusion_cte_names[0] else "AND"
   108→        )
   109→        parts.append(
   110→            f"{prefix} NOT EXISTS (\n"
   111→            f"    SELECT 1 FROM {cte_name} c\n"
   112→            f"    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id\n"
   113→            f")"
   114→        )
   115→
   116→    parts.append(";")
   117→    return "\n".join(parts)
   118→
   119→
   120→def _get_valid_concept_id(atomic: AtomicCriterion) -> int | None:
   121→    """Get a validated integer concept ID for SQL interpolation."""
   122→    for val in (atomic.omop_concept_id, atomic.entity_concept_id):
   123→        if val is not None:
   124→            try:
   125→                return int(val)
   126→            except (ValueError, TypeError):
   127→                continue
   128→    return None
   129→
   130→
   131→def _build_atomic_cte(
   132→    atomic: AtomicCriterion, concept_id: int, cte_name: str
   133→) -> str:
   134→    """Build a CTE for a single atomic criterion."""
   135→    domain = (atomic.entity_domain or "condition").lower()
   136→
   137→    # Demographics: special handling for age
   138→    if domain == "demographics":
   139→        return _build_demographics_cte(atomic, cte_name)
   140→
   141→    table_name, concept_col = _DOMAIN_TABLE.get(
   142→        domain, ("condition_occurrence", "condition_concept_id")
   143→    )
   144→
   145→    lines: list[str] = []
   146→    lines.append(f"{cte_name} AS (")
   147→    lines.append("    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id")
   148→    lines.append(f"    FROM {table_name} t")
   149→    lines.append("    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca")
   150→    lines.append(f"        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.{concept_col}")
   151→    lines.append(f"    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = {concept_id}")
   152→
   153→    # Measurement value filter
   154→    if domain == "measurement" and atomic.relation_operator and atomic.value_numeric is not None:
   155→        sql_op = _SQL_OP.get(atomic.relation_operator, "=")
   156→        lines.append(
   157→            f"    AND t.value_as_number {sql_op} {atomic.value_numeric}"
   158→        )
   159→        if atomic.unit_concept_id is not None:
   160→            lines.append(
   161→                f"    AND t.unit_concept_id = {atomic.unit_concept_id}"
   162→            )
   163→
   164→    lines.append(")")
   165→    return "\n".join(lines)
   166→
   167→
   168→def _build_demographics_cte(
   169→    atomic: AtomicCriterion, cte_name: str
   170→) -> str:
   171→    """Build a CTE for demographics-based criteria (age)."""
   172→    lines: list[str] = []
   173→    lines.append(f"{cte_name} AS (")
   174→    lines.append("    SELECT p.person_id")
   175→    lines.append("    FROM person p")
   176→
   177→    if atomic.relation_operator and atomic.value_numeric is not None:
   178→        sql_op = _SQL_OP.get(atomic.relation_operator, "=")
   179→        lines.append(
   180→            f"    WHERE EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE) - p.year_of_birth"
   181→            f" {sql_op} {int(atomic.value_numeric)}"
   182→        )
   183→    else:
   184→        lines.append("    WHERE 1=1")
   185→
   186→    lines.append(")")
   187→    return "\n".join(lines)
   188→
```
