     1→"""OHDSI CIRCE CohortExpression JSON builder.
     2→
     3→Converts structured criteria (expression trees with atomic/composite nodes)
     4→into the CIRCE JSON format consumed by OHDSI Atlas and WebAPI.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from typing import Any
    10→
    11→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion
    12→
    13→from api_service.exporters import ProtocolExportData
    14→
    15→# Map our entity_domain values to CIRCE domain table names
    16→_DOMAIN_TO_CIRCE: dict[str, str] = {
    17→    "condition": "ConditionOccurrence",
    18→    "measurement": "Measurement",
    19→    "drug": "DrugExposure",
    20→    "procedure": "ProcedureOccurrence",
    21→    "observation": "Observation",
    22→    "device": "DeviceExposure",
    23→    "visit": "VisitOccurrence",
    24→}
    25→
    26→# Map relation_operator to CIRCE Op string
    27→_OP_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    28→    ">": "gt",
    29→    ">=": "gte",
    30→    "<": "lt",
    31→    "<=": "lte",
    32→    "=": "eq",
    33→    "==": "eq",
    34→    "!=": "neq",
    35→    "gt": "gt",
    36→    "gte": "gte",
    37→    "lt": "lt",
    38→    "lte": "lte",
    39→    "eq": "eq",
    40→    "neq": "neq",
    41→}
    42→
    43→
    44→def build_circe_export(data: ProtocolExportData) -> dict[str, Any]:
    45→    """Build a CIRCE CohortExpression JSON from structured criteria.
    46→
    47→    Args:
    48→        data: Loaded protocol export data.
    49→
    50→    Returns:
    51→        Dict representing the CIRCE CohortExpression.
    52→    """
    53→    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    54→    concept_set_index: dict[str, int] = {}  # concept_id -> index in concept_sets
    55→
    56→    inclusion_criteria: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    57→    censoring_criteria: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    58→
    59→    for criterion in data.criteria:
    60→        tree = criterion.structured_criterion
    61→        if not tree:
    62→            continue
    63→
    64→        is_exclusion = criterion.criteria_type == "exclusion"
    65→        group = _build_criteria_group(
    66→            tree, data, concept_sets, concept_set_index
    67→        )
    68→        if group is None:
    69→            continue
    70→
    71→        if is_exclusion:
    72→            censoring_criteria.append(group)
    73→        else:
    74→            inclusion_criteria.append(group)
    75→
    76→    expression: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "ConceptSets": concept_sets,
    78→        "PrimaryCriteria": {
    79→            "CriteriaList": [],
    80→            "ObservationWindow": {
    81→                "PriorDays": 0,
    82→                "PostDays": 0,
    83→            },
    84→            "PrimaryCriteriaLimit": {"Type": "First"},
    85→        },
    86→        "AdditionalCriteria": {
    87→            "Type": "ALL",
    88→            "CriteriaList": inclusion_criteria,
    89→        },
    90→        "CensoringCriteria": censoring_criteria,
    91→    }
    92→
    93→    return expression
    94→
    95→
    96→def _get_concept_id(atomic: AtomicCriterion) -> int | None:
    97→    """Get the best concept ID for an atomic criterion.
    98→
    99→    Prefers omop_concept_id, falls back to entity_concept_id.
   100→    Returns None if neither is a valid integer.
   101→    """
   102→    for field_val in (atomic.omop_concept_id, atomic.entity_concept_id):
   103→        if field_val is not None:
   104→            try:
   105→                return int(field_val)
   106→            except (ValueError, TypeError):
   107→                continue
   108→    return None
   109→
   110→
   111→def _ensure_concept_set(
   112→    atomic: AtomicCriterion,
   113→    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]],
   114→    concept_set_index: dict[str, int],
   115→) -> int | None:
   116→    """Ensure a ConceptSet exists for the atomic's concept ID.
   117→
   118→    Returns the ConceptSet index, or None if no valid concept ID.
   119→    """
   120→    concept_id = _get_concept_id(atomic)
   121→    if concept_id is None:
   122→        return None
   123→
   124→    key = str(concept_id)
   125→    if key in concept_set_index:
   126→        return concept_set_index[key]
   127→
   128→    idx = len(concept_sets)
   129→    concept_sets.append({
   130→        "id": idx,
   131→        "name": atomic.original_text or f"Concept {concept_id}",
   132→        "expression": {
   133→            "items": [
   134→                {
   135→                    "concept": {
   136→                        "CONCEPT_ID": concept_id,
   137→                        "CONCEPT_NAME": atomic.original_text or "",
   138→                    },
   139→                    "isExcluded": False,
   140→                    "includeDescendants": True,
   141→                    "includeMapped": False,
   142→                }
   143→            ],
   144→        },
   145→    })
   146→    concept_set_index[key] = idx
   147→    return idx
   148→
   149→
   150→def _build_atomic_criteria(
   151→    atomic: AtomicCriterion,
   152→    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]],
   153→    concept_set_index: dict[str, int],
   154→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   155→    """Build a CIRCE criteria entry from an AtomicCriterion."""
   156→    cs_idx = _ensure_concept_set(atomic, concept_sets, concept_set_index)
   157→    if cs_idx is None:
   158→        return None
   159→
   160→    domain = _DOMAIN_TO_CIRCE.get(
   161→        (atomic.entity_domain or "").lower(), "ConditionOccurrence"
   162→    )
   163→
   164→    criteria_entry: dict[str, Any] = {
   165→        "Criteria": {
   166→            domain: {
   167→                "CodesetId": cs_idx,
   168→            },
   169→        },
   170→    }
   171→
   172→    # Handle negation: absence = OccurrenceCount of 0
   173→    if atomic.negation:
   174→        criteria_entry["Criteria"]["OccurrenceCount"] = {
   175→            "Value": 0,
   176→            "Op": "eq",
   177→        }
   178→
   179→    # Add value filter for measurements
   180→    if atomic.relation_operator and atomic.value_numeric is not None:
   181→        op_str = _OP_MAP.get(atomic.relation_operator, "eq")
   182→        value_filter: dict[str, Any] = {
   183→            "Value": atomic.value_numeric,
   184→            "Op": op_str,
   185→        }
   186→        if atomic.unit_concept_id is not None:
   187→            value_filter["UnitConceptId"] = atomic.unit_concept_id
   188→        criteria_entry["Criteria"]["ValueAsNumber"] = value_filter
   189→
   190→    return criteria_entry
   191→
   192→
   193→def _build_criteria_group(
   194→    tree: dict[str, Any],
   195→    data: ProtocolExportData,
   196→    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]],
   197→    concept_set_index: dict[str, int],
   198→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   199→    """Recursively build a CIRCE CriteriaGroup from an expression tree node.
   200→
   201→    The tree uses ExpressionNode format:
   202→    - type: AND/OR/NOT/ATOMIC
   203→    - atomic_criterion_id: FK for ATOMIC nodes
   204→    - children: list of child nodes for AND/OR/NOT
   205→    """
   206→    node_type = tree.get("type", "").upper()
   207→
   208→    if node_type == "ATOMIC":
   209→        atomic_id = tree.get("atomic_criterion_id")
   210→        if not atomic_id:
   211→            return None
   212→        atomic = data.atomics_by_id.get(atomic_id)
   213→        if not atomic:
   214→            return None
   215→        return _build_atomic_criteria(atomic, concept_sets, concept_set_index)
   216→
   217→    if node_type in ("AND", "OR"):
   218→        children = tree.get("children", [])
   219→        child_criteria = []
   220→        for child in children:
   221→            result = _build_criteria_group(
   222→                child, data, concept_sets, concept_set_index
   223→            )
   224→            if result is not None:
   225→                child_criteria.append(result)
   226→
   227→        if not child_criteria:
   228→            return None
   229→
   230→        group_type = "ALL" if node_type == "AND" else "ANY"
   231→        return {
   232→            "Type": group_type,
   233→            "CriteriaList": child_criteria,
   234→        }
   235→
   236→    if node_type == "NOT":
   237→        children = tree.get("children", [])
   238→        if not children:
   239→            return None
   240→        inner = _build_criteria_group(
   241→            children[0], data, concept_sets, concept_set_index
   242→        )
   243→        if inner is None:
   244→            return None
   245→        # Wrap in negated occurrence
   246→        if "Criteria" in inner:
   247→            inner["Criteria"]["OccurrenceCount"] = {
   248→                "Value": 0,
   249→                "Op": "eq",
   250→            }
   251→        return inner
   252→
   253→    return None
   254→
```

     1→"""FHIR R4 Group resource builder (EBM IG).
     2→
     3→Converts structured criteria into a FHIR Group resource with
     4→characteristics representing eligibility criteria.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from typing import Any
    10→from uuid import uuid4
    11→
    12→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion
    13→
    14→from api_service.exporters import ProtocolExportData
    15→
    16→# Map entity_concept_system to FHIR system URIs
    17→_SYSTEM_URI_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    18→    "snomed": "http://snomed.info/sct",
    19→    "snomedct": "http://snomed.info/sct",
    20→    "loinc": "http://loinc.org",
    21→    "rxnorm": "http://www.nlm.nih.gov/research/umls/rxnorm",
    22→    "icd10": "http://hl7.org/fhir/sid/icd-10",
    23→    "icd10cm": "http://hl7.org/fhir/sid/icd-10-cm",
    24→    "cpt": "http://www.ama-assn.org/go/cpt",
    25→    "hcpcs": "urn:oid:2.16.840.1.113883.6.285",
    26→    "omop": "http://ohdsi.org/omop/concept",
    27→}
    28→
    29→# Map our relation_operator to FHIR comparator
    30→_COMPARATOR_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    31→    ">": "gt",
    32→    ">=": "ge",
    33→    "<": "lt",
    34→    "<=": "le",
    35→    "gt": "gt",
    36→    "gte": "ge",
    37→    "lt": "lt",
    38→    "lte": "le",
    39→}
    40→
    41→
    42→def build_fhir_group_export(data: ProtocolExportData) -> dict[str, Any]:
    43→    """Build a FHIR R4 Group resource from structured criteria.
    44→
    45→    Args:
    46→        data: Loaded protocol export data.
    47→
    48→    Returns:
    49→        Dict representing the FHIR Group resource.
    50→    """
    51→    characteristics: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    52→
    53→    for criterion in data.criteria:
    54→        tree = criterion.structured_criterion
    55→        if not tree:
    56→            continue
    57→
    58→        is_exclusion = criterion.criteria_type == "exclusion"
    59→        chars = _build_characteristics_from_tree(tree, data, exclude=is_exclusion)
    60→        characteristics.extend(chars)
    61→
    62→    group: dict[str, Any] = {
    63→        "resourceType": "Group",
    64→        "id": data.protocol.id,
    65→        "type": "person",
    66→        "actual": False,
    67→        "name": data.protocol.title,
    68→        "characteristic": characteristics,
    69→    }
    70→
    71→    # Set combination method if we have characteristics
    72→    if characteristics:
    73→        group["extension"] = [
    74→            {
    75→                "url": (
    76→                    "http://hl7.org/fhir/uv/ebm/StructureDefinition"
    77→                    "/characteristic-combination"
    78→                ),
    79→                "valueCode": "all-of",
    80→            }
    81→        ]
    82→
    83→    return group
    84→
    85→
    86→def _build_characteristics_from_tree(
    87→    tree: dict[str, Any],
    88→    data: ProtocolExportData,
    89→    exclude: bool = False,
    90→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    91→    """Recursively build FHIR characteristics from an expression tree node."""
    92→    node_type = tree.get("type", "").upper()
    93→
    94→    if node_type == "ATOMIC":
    95→        atomic_id = tree.get("atomic_criterion_id")
    96→        if not atomic_id:
    97→            return []
    98→        atomic = data.atomics_by_id.get(atomic_id)
    99→        if not atomic:
   100→            return []
   101→        char = _build_characteristic(atomic, exclude=exclude)
   102→        return [char] if char else []
   103→
   104→    if node_type == "AND":
   105→        children = tree.get("children", [])
   106→        result = []
   107→        for child in children:
   108→            result.extend(
   109→                _build_characteristics_from_tree(child, data, exclude=exclude)
   110→            )
   111→        return result
   112→
   113→    if node_type == "OR":
   114→        children = tree.get("children", [])
   115→        # OR requires a nested contained Group with any-of combination
   116→        or_chars: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   117→        for child in children:
   118→            or_chars.extend(
   119→                _build_characteristics_from_tree(child, data, exclude=exclude)
   120→            )
   121→        if not or_chars:
   122→            return []
   123→
   124→        nested_group_id = str(uuid4())
   125→        nested_group: dict[str, Any] = {
   126→            "resourceType": "Group",
   127→            "id": nested_group_id,
   128→            "type": "person",
   129→            "actual": False,
   130→            "characteristic": or_chars,
   131→            "extension": [
   132→                {
   133→                    "url": (
   134→                        "http://hl7.org/fhir/uv/ebm/StructureDefinition"
   135→                        "/characteristic-combination"
   136→                    ),
   137→                    "valueCode": "any-of",
   138→                }
   139→            ],
   140→        }
   141→        return [
   142→            {
   143→                "code": {
   144→                    "text": "Nested OR group",
   145→                },
   146→                "valueReference": {
   147→                    "reference": f"#/{nested_group_id}",
   148→                },
   149→                "exclude": exclude,
   150→                "_contained": nested_group,
   151→            }
   152→        ]
   153→
   154→    if node_type == "NOT":
   155→        children = tree.get("children", [])
   156→        if not children:
   157→            return []
   158→        # Invert the exclude flag
   159→        return _build_characteristics_from_tree(
   160→            children[0], data, exclude=not exclude
   161→        )
   162→
   163→    return []
   164→
   165→
   166→def _get_system_uri(system: str | None) -> str:
   167→    """Map an entity_concept_system to a FHIR system URI."""
   168→    if not system:
   169→        return "http://ohdsi.org/omop/concept"
   170→    return _SYSTEM_URI_MAP.get(system.lower(), f"urn:oid:{system}")
   171→
   172→
   173→def _get_concept_code(atomic: AtomicCriterion) -> str | None:
   174→    """Get the best concept code for an atomic criterion."""
   175→    for val in (atomic.omop_concept_id, atomic.entity_concept_id):
   176→        if val is not None:
   177→            return str(val)
   178→    return None
   179→
   180→
   181→def _build_characteristic(
   182→    atomic: AtomicCriterion,
   183→    exclude: bool = False,
   184→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   185→    """Build a single FHIR Group characteristic from an AtomicCriterion."""
   186→    concept_code = _get_concept_code(atomic)
   187→    if concept_code is None:
   188→        return None
   189→
   190→    system_uri = _get_system_uri(atomic.entity_concept_system)
   191→
   192→    char: dict[str, Any] = {
   193→        "code": {
   194→            "coding": [
   195→                {
   196→                    "system": system_uri,
   197→                    "code": concept_code,
   198→                    "display": atomic.original_text or "",
   199→                }
   200→            ],
   201→        },
   202→        "exclude": exclude or atomic.negation,
   203→    }
   204→
   205→    # Add value: quantity or text
   206→    if atomic.value_numeric is not None:
   207→        quantity: dict[str, Any] = {
   208→            "value": atomic.value_numeric,
   209→        }
   210→        if atomic.unit_text:
   211→            quantity["unit"] = atomic.unit_text
   212→        if atomic.relation_operator:
   213→            comparator = _COMPARATOR_MAP.get(atomic.relation_operator)
   214→            if comparator:
   215→                quantity["comparator"] = comparator
   216→        char["valueQuantity"] = quantity
   217→    elif atomic.value_text:
   218→        char["valueCodeableConcept"] = {
   219→            "text": atomic.value_text,
   220→        }
   221→    else:
   222→        # Boolean presence/absence
   223→        char["valueBoolean"] = not atomic.negation
   224→
   225→    return char
   226→
```

     1→"""Real E2E: ordinal resolution with live Gemini + live Postgres.
     2→
     3→Creates a protocol with mixed criteria (known ordinal, unknown ordinal,
     4→lab values), runs build_expression_tree to create AtomicCriterion
     5→records, then runs ordinal_resolve_node with the REAL Gemini API.
     6→
     7→Usage:
     8→    set -a && source ../../.env && set +a
     9→    uv run python scripts/run_ordinal_e2e.py
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→import asyncio
    15→import json
    16→from typing import Any, TypedDict, cast
    17→
    18→from api_service.storage import engine
    19→from shared.models import (
    20→    AtomicCriterion,
    21→    AuditLog,
    22→    Criteria,
    23→    CriteriaBatch,
    24→    Protocol,
    25→)
    26→from sqlmodel import Session, select
    27→
    28→from protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve import (
    29→    ordinal_resolve_node,
    30→)
    31→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    32→from protocol_processor.tools.structure_builder import (
    33→    build_expression_tree,
    34→)
    35→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import (
    36→    normalize_ordinal_value,
    37→)
    38→
    39→
    40→class _FieldMapping(TypedDict, total=False):
    41→    """One field mapping for build_expression_tree."""
    42→
    43→    entity: str
    44→    relation: str
    45→    value: str
    46→    unit: str | None
    47→
    48→
    49→class _CriterionDef(TypedDict):
    50→    """One test criterion with text, label, and mappings."""
    51→
    52→    text: str
    53→    label: str
    54→    mappings: list[_FieldMapping]
    55→
    56→
    57→# ── Test criteria: mix of known, unknown, and non-ordinal ─────────
    58→
    59→CRITERIA: list[_CriterionDef] = [
    60→    {
    61→        "text": "ECOG performance status <= 2",
    62→        "label": "ECOG (known ordinal)",
    63→        "mappings": [
    64→            {
    65→                "entity": "ECOG performance status",
    66→                "relation": "<=",
    67→                "value": "2",
    68→                "unit": None,
    69→            },
    70→        ],
    71→    },
    72→    {
    73→        "text": "Child-Pugh score <= 6 (class A)",
    74→        "label": "Child-Pugh (unknown ordinal)",
    75→        "mappings": [
    76→            {
    77→                "entity": "Child-Pugh score",
    78→                "relation": "<=",
    79→                "value": "6",
    80→                "unit": None,
    81→            },
    82→        ],
    83→    },
    84→    {
    85→        "text": "Glasgow Coma Scale >= 13",
    86→        "label": "GCS (unknown ordinal)",
    87→        "mappings": [
    88→            {
    89→                "entity": "Glasgow Coma Scale",
    90→                "relation": ">=",
    91→                "value": "13",
    92→                "unit": None,
    93→            },
    94→        ],
    95→    },
    96→    {
    97→        "text": "MELD score <= 15",
    98→        "label": "MELD (unknown ordinal)",
    99→        "mappings": [
   100→            {
   101→                "entity": "MELD score",
   102→                "relation": "<=",
   103→                "value": "15",
   104→                "unit": None,
   105→            },
   106→        ],
   107→    },
   108→    {
   109→        "text": "HbA1c <= 7.0%",
   110→        "label": "HbA1c (lab value, not ordinal)",
   111→        "mappings": [
   112→            {
   113→                "entity": "HbA1c",
   114→                "relation": "<=",
   115→                "value": "7.0",
   116→                "unit": "%",
   117→            },
   118→        ],
   119→    },
   120→    {
   121→        "text": "Creatinine clearance >= 60 mL/min",
   122→        "label": "CrCl (lab value, not ordinal)",
   123→        "mappings": [
   124→            {
   125→                "entity": "Creatinine clearance",
   126→                "relation": ">=",
   127→                "value": "60",
   128→                "unit": "mL/min",
   129→            },
   130→        ],
   131→    },
   132→]
   133→
   134→
   135→def _sep(title: str) -> None:
   136→    print(f"\n{'=' * 60}")
   137→    print(f"  {title}")
   138→    print(f"{'=' * 60}\n")
   139→
   140→
   141→def _phase1_create(session: Session) -> tuple[str, str]:
   142→    """Create protocol + batch and build expression trees."""
   143→    protocol = Protocol(
   144→        title="ORDINAL-E2E-LIVE",
   145→        file_uri="local://ordinal-e2e.pdf",
   146→    )
   147→    session.add(protocol)
   148→    session.flush()
   149→    batch = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol.id)
   150→    session.add(batch)
   151→    session.flush()
   152→    return protocol.id, batch.id
   153→
   154→
   155→async def _phase1_build(
   156→    session: Session,
   157→    protocol_id: str,
   158→    batch_id: str,
   159→) -> None:
   160→    """Process each criterion through build_expression_tree."""
   161→    for crit_def in CRITERIA:
   162→        crit = Criteria(
   163→            batch_id=batch_id,
   164→            criteria_type="inclusion",
   165→            text=crit_def["text"],
   166→        )
   167→        session.add(crit)
   168→        session.flush()
   169→
   170→        await build_expression_tree(
   171→            criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   172→            field_mappings=cast(list[dict[str, Any]], crit_def["mappings"]),
   173→            criterion_id=crit.id,
   174→            protocol_id=protocol_id,
   175→            inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   176→            session=session,
   177→        )
   178→        session.flush()
   179→
   180→        atomics = session.exec(
   181→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   182→                AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit.id,
   183→            )
   184→        ).all()
   185→        a = atomics[0] if atomics else None
   186→
   187→        ordinal_check = normalize_ordinal_value(
   188→            str(crit_def["mappings"][0].get("value", "")),
   189→            crit_def["mappings"][0].get("entity"),
   190→        )
   191→
   192→        label = crit_def["label"]
   193→        if a:
   194→            print(
   195→                f"  {label}:"
   196→                f"  unit_concept_id={a.unit_concept_id}"
   197→                f"  value_numeric={a.value_numeric}"
   198→                f"  unit_text={a.unit_text!r}"
   199→                f"  ordinal_match={'YES' if ordinal_check else 'no'}"
   200→            )
   201→        else:
   202→            print(f"  {label}: NO ATOMIC CREATED")
   203→
   204→    session.commit()
   205→
   206→
   207→def _print_proposals(result: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   208→    """Print Gemini proposals from result state."""
   209→    proposals_json = result.get("ordinal_proposals_json")
   210→    if not proposals_json:
   211→        print("\nNo proposals returned (LLM may have failed)")
   212→        return
   213→
   214→    proposals = json.loads(proposals_json)
   215→    print(f"\nProposals from Gemini ({len(proposals)}):")
   216→    for p in proposals:
   217→        print(f"\n  Scale: {p.get('scale_name')}")
   218→        print(f"  Entity: {p.get('entity_text')}")
   219→        print(f"  Confidence: {p.get('confidence')}")
   220→        print(f"  LOINC: {p.get('loinc_code')}")
   221→        print(f"  Aliases: {p.get('entity_aliases', [])}")
   222→        values = p.get("values", [])
   223→        if values:
   224→            print(f"  Values ({len(values)}):")
   225→            for v in values[:5]:
   226→                print(
   227→                    f"    {v.get('grade')}: {v.get('description')}"
   228→                    f" (SNOMED: {v.get('snomed_code')})"
   229→                )
   230→            if len(values) > 5:
   231→                print(f"    ... and {len(values) - 5} more")
   232→
   233→
   234→def _phase3_verify(batch_id: str) -> None:
   235→    """Verify DB state after ordinal resolution."""
   236→    with Session(engine) as session:
   237→        from sqlalchemy.sql.expression import ColumnElement
   238→
   239→        on_clause = cast(
   240→            ColumnElement[bool],
   241→            AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == Criteria.id,
   242→        )
   243→        atomics = session.exec(
   244→            select(AtomicCriterion)
   245→            .join(Criteria, on_clause)
   246→            .where(Criteria.batch_id == batch_id)
   247→        ).all()
   248→
   249→        print(f"AtomicCriteria in batch: {len(atomics)}\n")
   250→        for a in atomics:
   251→            marker = ""
   252→            if a.unit_concept_id == 8527:
   253→                marker = " << {score}"
   254→            elif a.unit_concept_id is not None:
   255→                marker = f" << unit={a.unit_concept_id}"
   256→            print(
   257→                f"  {a.original_text!r}"
   258→                f"\n    unit_concept_id={a.unit_concept_id}"
   259→                f"  value_numeric={a.value_numeric}"
   260→                f"  unit_text={a.unit_text!r}{marker}"
   261→            )
   262→            print()
   263→
   264→        audits = session.exec(
   265→            select(AuditLog).where(
   266→                AuditLog.event_type == "ordinal_scale_proposed",
   267→                AuditLog.target_id == batch_id,
   268→            )
   269→        ).all()
   270→        if audits:
   271→            print(f"AuditLog entries: {len(audits)}")
   272→            for audit in audits:
   273→                details = audit.details
   274→                print(
   275→                    f"  candidates_found={details.get('candidates_found')}"
   276→                    f"  updated={details.get('updated_count')}"
   277→                    f"  proposals="
   278→                    f"{len(details.get('proposals', []))}"
   279→                )
   280→        else:
   281→            print("No AuditLog entries found")
   282→
   283→
   284→async def main() -> None:
   285→    """Run full E2E ordinal resolution pipeline."""
   286→    _sep("PHASE 1: Create protocol & build expression trees")
   287→
   288→    with Session(engine) as session:
   289→        protocol_id, batch_id = _phase1_create(session)
   290→        print(f"Protocol: {protocol_id}")
   291→        print(f"Batch:    {batch_id}")
   292→        await _phase1_build(session, protocol_id, batch_id)
   293→
   294→    _sep("PHASE 2: Run ordinal_resolve_node (REAL Gemini)")
   295→
   296→    state: PipelineState = {
   297→        "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   298→        "file_uri": "",
   299→        "title": "ORDINAL-E2E-LIVE",
   300→        "batch_id": batch_id,
   301→        "pdf_bytes": None,
   302→        "extraction_json": None,
   303→        "entities_json": None,
   304→        "grounded_entities_json": None,
   305→        "archived_reviewed_criteria": None,
   306→        "ordinal_proposals_json": None,
   307→        "status": "processing",
   308→        "error": None,
   309→        "errors": [],
   310→    }
   311→
   312→    result = await ordinal_resolve_node(state)
   313→
   314→    print(f"Status: {result.get('status')}")
   315→    print(f"Errors: {result.get('errors', [])}")
   316→    _print_proposals(result)
   317→
   318→    _sep("PHASE 3: Verify DB state")
   319→    _phase3_verify(batch_id)
   320→
   321→    _sep("DONE")
   322→
   323→
   324→if __name__ == "__main__":
   325→    asyncio.run(main())
   326→

     \"value_numeric\": 10.0,\n                \"unit_text\": \"%\",\n                \"unit_concept_id\": 8554,\n                \"original_text\": \"HbA1c <= 10.0%\",\n            },\n        ],\n    },\n    {\n        \"type\": \"inclusion\",\n        \"text\": \"eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2\",\n        \"atomics\": [\n            {\n                \"entity_domain\": \"measurement\",\n                \"entity_concept_system\": \"loinc\",\n                \"entity_concept_id\": \"62238-1\",\n                \"omop_concept_id\": \"3049187\",\n                \"relation_operator\": \">=\",\n                \"value_numeric\": 30.0,\n                \"unit_text\": \"mL/min/1.73m2\",\n                \"unit_concept_id\": 8795,\n                \"original_text\": \"eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2\",\n            },\n        ],\n    },\n    # ── Exclusion ───\n    {\n        \"type\": \"exclusion\",\n        \"text\": \"Type 1 diabetes mellitus\",\n        \"atomics\": [\n            {\n                \"entity_domain\": \"condition\",\n                \"entity_concept_system\": \"snomed\",\n                \"entity_concept_id\": \"46635009\",\n                \"omop_concept_id\": \"201254\",\n                \"negation\": True,\n                \"original_text\": \"Type 1 diabetes mellitus\",\n            },\n        ],\n    },\n    {\n        \"type\": \"exclusion\",\n        \"text\": \"Current use of insulin or GLP-1 receptor agonist\",\n        \"logic\": \"OR\",\n        \"atomics\": [\n            {\n                \"entity_domain\": \"drug\",\n                \"entity_concept_system\": \"rxnorm\",\n                \"entity_concept_id\": \"253182\",\n                \"omop_concept_id\": \"1567198\",\n                \"original_text\": \"Insulin\",\n            },\n            {\n                \"entity_domain\": \"drug\",\n                \"entity_concept_system\": \"rxnorm\",\n                \"entity_concept_id\": \"60548\",\n                \"omop_concept_id\": \"1583722\",\n                \"original_text\": \"GLP-1 receptor agonist\",\n            },\n        ],\n    },\n    {\n        \"type\": \"exclusion\",\n        \"text\": \"NYHA class III or IV heart failure\",\n        \"atomics\": [\n            {\n                \"entity_domain\": \"condition\",\n                \"entity_concept_system\": \"snomed\",\n                \"entity_concept_id\": \"195111005\",\n                \"omop_concept_id\": \"316139\",\n                \"value_text\": \"Class III-IV\",\n                \"original_text\": \"NYHA class III/IV heart failure\",\n            },\n        ],\n    },\n]\n\n\ndef _seed(session: Session) -> ProtocolExportData:\n    \"\"\"Seed the database and return export data.\"\"\"\n    proto = Protocol(\n        id=str(uuid4()),\n        title=\"Phase 3 T2DM SGLT2 Inhibitor Trial (NCT00000000)\",\n        file_uri=\"local://demo.pdf\",\n        status=\"complete\",\n    )\n    session.add(proto)\n    session.flush()\n\n    batch = CriteriaBatch(\n        id=str(uuid4()),\n        protocol_id=proto.id,\n        is_archived=False,\n    )\n    session.add(batch)\n    session.flush()\n\n    all_criteria = []\n    all_atomics = []\n    all_composites = []\n    all_rels = []\n\n    for cdef in CRITERIA_DEFS:\n        atomic_ids = [str(uuid4()) for _ in cdef[\"atomics\"]]\n        logic = cdef.get(\"logic\")\n\n        # Build expression tree\n        if len(atomic_ids) == 1:\n            tree = {\n                \"type\": \"ATOMIC\",\n                \"atomic_criterion_id\": atomic_ids[0],\n            }\n        else:\n            tree = {\n                \"type\": logic or \"AND\",\n                \"children\": [\n                    {\"type\": \"ATOMIC\", \"atomic_criterion_id\": aid}\n                    for aid in atomic_ids\n                ],\n            }\n\n        crit = Criteria(\n            id=str(uuid4()),\n            batch_id=batch.id,\n            criteria_type=cdef[\"type\"],\n            text=cdef[\"text\"],\n            structured_criterion=tree,\n        )\n        session.add(crit)\n        session.flush()\n        all_criteria.append(crit)\n\n        for i, (adef, aid) in enumerate(zip(cdef[\"atomics\"], atomic_ids)):\n            a = AtomicCriterion(\n                id=aid,\n                criterion_id=crit.id,\n                protocol_id=proto.id,\n                inclusion_exclusion=cdef[\"type\"],\n                entity_concept_id=adef.get(\"entity_concept_id\"),\n                entity_concept_system=adef.get(\"entity_concept_system\"),\n                omop_concept_id=adef.get(\"omop_concept_id\"),\n                entity_domain=adef.get(\"entity_domain\"),\n                relation_operator=adef.get(\"relation_operator\"),\n                value_numeric=adef.get(\"value_numeric\"),\n                value_text=adef.get(\"value_text\"),\n                unit_text=adef.get(\"unit_text\"),\n                unit_concept_id=adef.get(\"unit_concept_id\"),\n                negation=adef.get(\"negation\", False),\n                original_text=adef.get(\"original_text\"),\n                confidence_score=0.95,\n            )\n            session.add(a)\n            all_atomics.append(a)\n\n        # If composite, also make a composite + relationships\n        if logic and len(atomic_ids) > 1:\n            comp = CompositeCriterion(\n                id=str(uuid4()),\n                criterion_id=crit.id,\n                protocol_id=proto.id,\n                inclusion_exclusion=cdef[\"type\"],\n                logic_operator=logic,\n                original_text=cdef[\"text\"],\n            )\n            session.add(comp)\n            session.flush()\n            all_composites.append(comp)\n\n            for seq, aid in enumerate(atomic_ids):\n                rel = CriterionRelationship(\n                    parent_criterion_id=comp.id,\n                    child_criterion_id=aid,\n                    child_type=\"atomic\",\n                    child_sequence=seq,\n                )\n                session.add(rel)\n                all_rels.append(rel)\n\n    session.commit()\n\n    return ProtocolExportData(\n        protocol=proto,\n        criteria=all_criteria,\n        atomics=all_atomics,\n        composites=all_composites,\n        relationships=all_rels,\n        atomics_by_id={a.id: a for a in all_atomics},\n        composites_by_id={c.id: c for c in all_composites},\n        children_by_parent={},\n        criteria_by_id={c.id: c for c in all_criteria},\n    )\n\n\ndef _sep(title: str) -> None:\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 72}\")\n    print(f\"  {title}\")\n    print(f\"{'=' * 72}\")\n\n\ndef _print_criteria_table(data: ProtocolExportData) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Print a summary table of seeded criteria.\"\"\"\n    header = (\n        f\"{'#':<3} {'Type':<10} {'Domain':<14} {'Text':<35} \"\n        f\"{'OMOP':<10} {'Op':<4} {'Value':<8} {'Unit':<14} {'Neg':<4}\"\n    )\n    print(header)\n    print(\"-\" * len(header))\n    for i, a in enumerate(data.atomics, 1):\n        print(\n            f\"{i:<3} \"\n            f\"{a.inclusion_exclusion:<10} \"\n            f\"{(a.entity_domain or '-'):<14} \"\n            f\"{(a.original_text or '-')[:35]:<35} \"\n            f\"{(a.omop_concept_id or '-'):<10} \"\n            f\"{(a.relation_operator or '-'):<4} \"\n            f\"{str(a.value_numeric or a.value_text or '-'):<8} \"\n            f\"{(a.unit_text or '-'):<14} \"\n            f\"{'Y' if a.negation else 'N':<4}\"\n        )\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Seed data and display export outputs.\"\"\"\n    engine = create_engine(\n        \"sqlite:///:memory:\",\n        connect_args={\"check_same_thread\": False},\n        poolclass=StaticPool,\n    )\n    SQLModel.metadata.create_all(engine)\n\n    with Session(engine) as session:\n        data = _seed(session)\n\n    # ── Table ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    _sep(\"SEEDED ATOMIC CRITERIA\")\n    _print_criteria_table(data)\n    print(\n        f\"\\nTotals: {len(data.criteria)} criteria, \"\n        f\"{len(data.atomics)} atomics, \"\n        f\"{len(data.composites)} composites, \"\n        f\"{len(data.relationships)} relationships\"\n    )\n\n    # ── CIRCE ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    _sep(\"CIRCE CohortExpression JSON\")\n    circe = build_circe_export(data)\n    print(json.dumps(circe, indent=2))\n\n    # ── FHIR Group ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n    _sep(\"FHIR R4 Group Resource JSON\")\n    fhir = build_fhir_group_export(data)\n    print(json.dumps(fhir, indent=2))\n\n    # ── Evaluation SQL ───────────────────────────────────────────\n    _sep(\"OMOP CDM v5.4 Evaluation SQL\")\n    sql = build_evaluation_sql(data)\n    print(sql)\n\n    _sep(\"DONE\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

  OMOP CDM v5.4 Evaluation SQL

-- Auto-generated OMOP CDM v5.4 eligibility evaluation SQL
-- Protocol: Phase 3 T2DM SGLT2 Inhibitor Trial (NCT00000000) (dea99052-de33-49d7-967c-34e8fcd3edce)
-- Generated from 9 atomic criteria

WITH
cte_0 AS (
    SELECT p.person_id
    FROM person p
    WHERE EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE) - p.year_of_birth >= 18
),

cte_1 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM condition_occurrence t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.condition_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 201826
),

cte_2 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM measurement t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.measurement_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 3004410
    AND t.value_as_number >= 7.0
    AND t.unit_concept_id = 8554
),

cte_3 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM measurement t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.measurement_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 3004410
    AND t.value_as_number <= 10.0
    AND t.unit_concept_id = 8554
),

cte_4 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM measurement t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.measurement_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 3049187
    AND t.value_as_number >= 30.0
    AND t.unit_concept_id = 8795
),

cte_5 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM condition_occurrence t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.condition_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 201254
),

cte_6 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM drug_exposure t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.drug_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 1567198
),

cte_7 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM drug_exposure t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.drug_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 1583722
),

cte_8 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM condition_occurrence t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.condition_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 316139
)


SELECT p.person_id
FROM person p
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_0 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_1 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_2 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_3 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_4 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_5 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_6 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_7 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_8 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
;

Here's the current state across all three exporters:

| Exporter | Demographics handling |
|---|---|
| **SQL** | Correct — special-cases `demographics` domain, uses `EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE) - p.year_of_birth` |
| **CIRCE** | Wrong — falls through to `ConditionOccurrence` (default domain), creates a ConceptSet for "age" |
| **FHIR** | Wrong — emits a characteristic with SNOMED code 4265453 as if age were a coded observation |

You're right — in OMOP CDM, age is **not** a concept you join on. `person.year_of_birth` is the source of truth. The standard representations handle this differently:

- **CIRCE** uses `DemographicCriteria` with an `Age` range filter (no ConceptSet needed at all)
- **FHIR EBM IG** uses a characteristic with code `http://terminology.hl7.org/CodeSystem/usage-context-type|age` and a `valueRange`

So the SQL builder already does it correctly, but CIRCE and FHIR are treating demographics like a regular concept-based domain, which would produce nonsense if someone tried to execute the CIRCE JSON against Atlas.

The question of whether to convert to `birthdate - today()` vs using the age value directly is format-specific:
- **SQL**: Already correct (derives age from `year_of_birth`)
- **CIRCE**: Should use `DemographicCriteria.Age` with `Value`/`Op` — Atlas handles the date math internally
- **FHIR**: Should use `valueRange` with low/high — the matcher handles date math

Want me to fix the CIRCE and FHIR builders to add proper demographics handling? It's a few lines in each — special-case `entity_domain == "demographics"` the same way the SQL builder already does.

## Complete Analysis: Age-Related Criteria Handling (Extraction through Grounding to Atomic Criteria)

### 1. EXTRACTION PHASE: How Age Criteria Are Identified

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/system.jinja2`

The extraction system prompt (lines 4, 6, 43-46, 51-73) instructs Gemini to:
- Assign category `"other"` (NOT `"demographics"`) to age criteria. Line 4 explicitly states: `Assign a category: medical_history, lab_values, medications, procedures, or other (NOT demographics -- demographic criteria are not grounded to terminology)`
- Extract numeric thresholds for age ranges. The prompt includes an explicit example (lines 51-73): `"Male or female, 40 years to 85 years old"` becomes `comparator: "range"`, `value: 40.0`, `upper_value: 85.0`, `unit: "years"`, and category is `"other"`.

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/schemas/extraction.py`

The `NumericThreshold` model (lines 59-94) captures age values with fields: `value`, `unit` (e.g. "years"), `comparator` (e.g. "range", ">="), and `upper_value`. The `ExtractedCriterion.category` field (lines 116-122) includes the description `"Do not use 'demographics' -- demographic criteria are not grounded."` -- but this is just an extraction hint; the entity type is determined later during decomposition.

**Key observation:** The extraction prompt tells Gemini to use category `"other"` for age criteria, but the extraction schema category field does NOT determine the entity type used for grounding. Entity type is determined separately in the decompose step.

### 2. ENTITY DECOMPOSITION: How "Age" Gets Its Entity Type

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/entity_decomposer.py`

The `DecomposedEntity` model (lines 27-38) has an `entity_type` field that is a `Literal["Condition", "Medication", "Lab_Value", "Procedure", "Demographic", "Other"]`. The `"Demographic"` type is one of the valid options.

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/entity_decompose.jinja2`

The entity decomposition prompt (line 8) includes a concrete example for age:
```
- "Age 20 to 60 years" -> text: "age", entity_type: "Demographic"
```

This means Gemini's entity decomposer will output `entity_type: "Demographic"` for age criteria, with the text being just `"age"` (the entity concept, not the full sentence with numbers).

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/parse.py`

In the parse node (lines 130-171), when decomposition succeeds, the entity items include `entity_type` from the decomposer output (e.g. `"Demographic"`). If decomposition fails, there's a fallback map (lines 157-162) where `"demographics"` (category) maps to `"Demographic"` (entity_type).

### 3. GROUNDING: How Age Gets Routed and Assigned SNOMED/OMOP Codes

#### 3a. Routing Configuration

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/config/routing.yaml`

Lines 10-12 and 43-45:
```yaml
# Note: Demographic entities are NOT skipped -- age/gender grounding is attempted
# via UMLS/SNOMED. The MedGemma agentic reasoning loop handles derived entity
# mapping (age -> birthDate concept) before retry attempts.

Demographic:
  - umls
  - snomed
```

Age entities (entity_type `"Demographic"`) are routed to both **UMLS** and **SNOMED** APIs via ToolUniverse. They are NOT skipped (unlike Consent entities).

#### 3b. ToolUniverse Search

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/tooluniverse_client.py`

For `"Demographic"` entities, the entity text `"age"` is searched against:
1. `umls_search_concepts` tool (UMLS REST API) -- lines 306-307
2. `snomed_search_concepts` tool (SNOMED via UMLS) -- lines 306-307

These return candidate codes for "age" which would include UMLS CUIs like `C0001779` (Age) and potentially SNOMED concepts accessible via UMLS cross-reference.

#### 3c. OMOP Mapper (Dual Grounding)

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py`

Lines 80-91 define the entity type to OMOP domain mapping:
```python
ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN: dict[str, str] = {
    "Condition": "Condition",
    "Medication": "Drug",
    "Lab_Value": "Measurement",
    "Procedure": "Procedure",
    "Demographic": "Observation",
}
```

`"Demographic"` entities are mapped to the `"Observation"` domain for OMOP concept table lookup. The OMOP mapper will search `concept` and `concept_synonym` tables where `domain_id = 'Observation'` and `standard_concept = 'S'` with `concept_name ILIKE '%age%'`.

#### 3d. MedGemma Decision + Agentic Retry

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/medgemma_decider.py`

MedGemma selects the best terminology code from the candidates returned by ToolUniverse. For age, the candidates would include UMLS concepts for "Age" and SNOMED concepts for "Age".

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/grounding_reasoning.jinja2`

Lines 22-27 -- the agentic reasoning prompt explicitly includes age as an example of a **derived entity**:
```
- "age >= 18 years" -> the concept is "age" (the numeric value is a filter, not the concept)
- "male" or "female" -> maps to SNOMED gender concepts
```

If initial grounding fails for "age", the agentic retry loop may identify it as a derived entity and use the derived term `"age"` for re-search, or rephrase to `"chronological age"` for better SNOMED matching.

#### 3e. Field Mapping Generation

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py`

The field mapper generates AutoCriteria decompositions via Gemini. For "Age >= 18 years", the prompt at line 116 gives examples like `'Age'` for entity, `'>='` for relation, `'18'` for value, `'years'` for unit.

The mapping output (lines 133-144) includes:
```python
{
    "entity": "Age",
    "relation": ">=",
    "value": "18",
    "unit": "years",
    "entity_concept_id": entity.selected_code,   # e.g. UMLS CUI from grounding
    "entity_concept_system": entity.selected_system,  # e.g. "umls" or "snomed"
    "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
}
```

**CRITICAL GAP: `entity_domain` is NOT populated in field_mapper.py output.** The field mapping dict does not include an `"entity_domain"` key. This is confirmed by my grep finding zero matches for `entity_domain` in `field_mapper.py`.

---

## Summary of the Complete Flow for Age Criteria

```
PDF -> [Gemini Extract] -> ExtractedCriterion
  - category: "other" (NOT "demographics")
  - numeric_thresholds: [{value: 18, unit: "years", comparator: ">="}]

-> [Gemini Decompose] -> DecomposedEntity
  - text: "age"
  - entity_type: "Demographic"

-> [TerminologyRouter] -> route to UMLS + SNOMED APIs
  - search "age" in UMLS: returns CUIs like C0001779
  - search "age" in SNOMED: returns SNOMED concepts via UMLS
  
-> [OMOP Mapper] -> domain = "Observation"
  - search "age" in concept table where domain_id='Observation'
  - returns OMOP concept_id (e.g. 4265453)

-> [MedGemma Decide] -> selects best code
  - selected_code: e.g. "C0001779" (UMLS) or SNOMED CUI
  - selected_system: "umls" or "snomed"
  
-> [Field Mapper] -> Gemini generates field_mappings
  - {entity: "Age", relation: ">=", value: "18", unit: "years",
     entity_concept_id: <code>, entity_concept_system: <system>,
     omop_concept_id: <omop_id>}
  - ** entity_domain is NOT included **

-> [Structure Builder] -> creates AtomicCriterion
  - entity_domain: None  (because field_mappings don't include it)
  - entity_concept_id: <from grounding>
  - omop_concept_id: <from OMOP mapper>
  - unit_concept_id: 9448 (from ucum_mappings.yaml for "years")
  - value_numeric: 18.0
  - relation_operator: ">="

-> [Export] -> entity_domain is None
  - CIRCE: defaults to "ConditionOccurrence" (WRONG for age)
  - Evaluation SQL: defaults to "condition" domain (WRONG for age)
  - Should use demographics domain with year_of_birth calculation
```

## Key Gap Identified

**`entity_domain` is never populated by the automated pipeline.** The `field_mapper.py` tool (lines 133-144) produces field_mapping dicts with `entity_concept_id`, `entity_concept_system`, and `omop_concept_id` but NOT `entity_domain`. The `structure_builder.py` reads `fm.get("entity_domain")` (line 217) which always returns `None`.

This means:
1. Age criteria exported via CIRCE will be incorrectly mapped to `ConditionOccurrence` instead of getting special demographic handling
2. Age criteria exported via Evaluation SQL will default to `"condition"` domain and query `condition_occurrence` table instead of using the `year_of_birth` calculation from the `person` table
3. The only place where `entity_domain = "demographics"` is correctly set is in the `demo_exports.py` script (hardcoded demo data)

To fix this, the `field_mapper.py` or `ground_node` needs to populate `entity_domain` in the field_mapping output based on the entity type (e.g. `"Demographic"` entity_type -> `"demographics"` entity_domain, `"Condition"` -> `"condition"`, `"Lab_Value"` -> `"measurement"`, etc.).

> AGENT

The research is very revealing. Here's the full picture:

## Grounding is correct — but `entity_domain` is never populated

The SNOMED/OMOP codes being assigned to age are fine:

| Source | Code | Concept |
|--------|------|---------|
| UMLS CUI | C0001779 | Age |
| SNOMED | 424144002 | Current chronological age |
| OMOP | 4265453 | Current chronological age (standard) |
| Unit | 9448 | Year (UCUM `"a"`) |

These correctly represent the *concept* of age in terminology systems. SNOMED 424144002 is the right observable entity concept for "what are we measuring" — the issue isn't the code, it's that the exporters don't know this is a demographics criterion.

## The real gap: `entity_domain` is always `None`

The pipeline flow for age is:

```
Extract → category: "other" (prompt says "NOT demographics")
Decompose → entity_type: "Demographic" ← correct
Grounding → UMLS/SNOMED search for "age" ← correct
OMOP Mapper → domain_id: "Observation" ← debatable
Field Mapper → {entity, relation, value, unit, concept_id, system, omop_id}
                ↑ entity_domain is NOT in this dict
Structure Builder → AtomicCriterion.entity_domain = fm.get("entity_domain") → None
```

`field_mapper.py` never emits `entity_domain`. `structure_builder.py` reads `fm.get("entity_domain")` which is always `None`. So downstream, all three exporters get `entity_domain=None` and default to condition-based logic — even though the decomposer correctly identified it as `"Demographic"`.

## What needs to happen

The fix is in `field_mapper.py` or `structure_builder.py` — the `entity_type` from decomposition (`"Demographic"`, `"Condition"`, `"Lab_Value"`, etc.) needs to be mapped to `entity_domain`:

| entity_type | entity_domain |
|-------------|---------------|
| Condition | condition |
| Lab_Value | measurement |
| Medication | drug |
| Procedure | procedure |
| Demographic | demographics |
| Other | observation |

This is a one-line mapping in the pipeline, not an export-layer concern. Once `entity_domain` is populated, the SQL builder's existing `_build_demographics_cte` (which already uses `year_of_birth`) will kick in, and the CIRCE/FHIR builders just need the same small special-case I mentioned earlier.

Want me to fix the pipeline to populate `entity_domain` from `entity_type`, and then add proper demographics handling to the CIRCE/FHIR builders?

    35→def _parse_value(
    36→    raw_value: str,
    37→) -> tuple[float | None, str | None]:

    41→    Otherwise returns (None, text).
    42→

    45→
    46→    Returns:
    47→        Tuple of (value_numeric, value_text) — one is always None.

    49→    try:
    50→        return (float(raw_value), None)
    51→    except (ValueError, TypeError):
    52→        return (None, raw_value)

    55→async def detect_logic_structure(
    56→    criterion_text: str,
    57→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
    58→) -> LogicDetectionResponse | None:
    59→    """Detect AND/OR/NOT logic structure in criterion text using Gemini.
    60→
    61→    Skips LLM call if there's only 1 field_mapping (single atom, no logic).
    62→    Returns None on any failure (triggers fallback in caller).
    63→
    64→    Args:
    65→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
    66→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
    67→
    68→    Returns:
    69→        LogicDetectionResponse with the detected tree, or None on failure.
    70→    """
    71→    if len(field_mappings) <= 1:
    72→        return None
    73→
    74→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
    75→    if not google_api_key:
    76→        logger.warning(
    77→            "GOOGLE_API_KEY not set — skipping logic detection for '%s'",
    78→            criterion_text[:50],
    79→        )
    80→        return None
    81→
    82→    try:
    83→        from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    84→
    85→        gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
    86→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    87→            model=gemini_model_name,
    88→            google_api_key=google_api_key,
    89→        )
    90→        structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(LogicDetectionResponse)
    91→
    92→        # Build field mapping descriptions for context
    93→        mapping_lines = []
    94→        for i, fm in enumerate(field_mappings):
    95→            entity = fm.get("entity", "?")
    96→            relation = fm.get("relation", "?")
    97→            value = fm.get("value", "?")
    98→            unit = fm.get("unit", "")
    99→            unit_str = f" {unit}" if unit else ""
   100→            mapping_lines.append(f"  [{i}] {entity} {relation} {value}{unit_str}")
   101→        mappings_text = "\n".join(mapping_lines)
   102→
   103→        prompt = (
   104→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Analyze the logical"
   105→            " structure of this eligibility criterion.\n\n"
   106→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   107→            f"Field mappings (indexed):\n{mappings_text}\n\n"
   108→            "Instructions:\n"
   109→            "- Determine how the field mappings are logically connected\n"
   110→            "- Use AND when all conditions must be met simultaneously\n"
   111→            "- Use OR when any one condition suffices\n"
   112→            "- Use NOT to negate a condition\n"
   113→            "- Use ATOMIC for leaf nodes that reference a single"
   114→            " field_mapping by index\n"
   115→            "- Return a tree where the root is AND, OR, NOT, or ATOMIC\n"
   116→            "- Every ATOMIC node must have field_mapping_index set to a"
   117→            " valid index (0 to "
   118→            f"{len(field_mappings) - 1})\n"
   119→            "- Each field_mapping index should appear exactly once\n"
   120→        )
   121→
   122→        result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   123→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   124→            response = LogicDetectionResponse.model_validate(result)
   125→        else:
   126→            response = result  # type: ignore[assignment]
   127→
   128→        # Validate all field_mapping_index values are in range
   129→        if not _validate_logic_tree(response.root, len(field_mappings)):
   130→            logger.warning(
   131→                "Logic tree validation failed for '%s' — invalid indices",
   132→                criterion_text[:50],
   133→            )
   134→            return None
   135→
   136→        return response
   137→
   138→    except Exception as e:
   139→        logger.warning(
   140→            "Logic detection failed for '%s': %s",
   141→            criterion_text[:50],
   142→            e,
   143→            exc_info=True,
   144→        )
   145→        return None

   147→
   148→def _validate_logic_tree(node: LogicNode, num_mappings: int) -> bool:
   149→    """Validate that all field_mapping_index values are in range.
   150→
   151→    Args:
   152→        node: Root LogicNode to validate.
   153→        num_mappings: Number of field_mappings (valid range: 0..num_mappings-1).
   154→
   155→    Returns:
   156→        True if all indices are valid, False otherwise.
   157→    """
   158→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   159→        if node.field_mapping_index is None:
   160→            return False
   161→        return 0 <= node.field_mapping_index < num_mappings
   162→    if node.children:
   163→        return all(_validate_logic_tree(child, num_mappings) for child in node.children)
   164→    # Non-ATOMIC with no children is invalid (AND/OR/NOT need children)
   165→    return False

   168→def _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   169→    fm: dict[str, Any],
   170→    criterion_id: str,
   171→    protocol_id: str,
   172→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   173→    criterion_text: str,
   174→) -> AtomicCriterion:
   175→    """Create an AtomicCriterion record from a field_mapping dict.
   176→
   177→    Args:
   178→        fm: Field mapping dict with entity, relation, value, unit keys.
   179→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   180→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   181→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   182→        criterion_text: Original criterion text.
   183→
   184→    Returns:
   185→        AtomicCriterion instance (not yet added to session).
   186→    """
   187→    value_str = fm.get("value", "")
   188→    value_numeric, value_text = _parse_value(value_str)
   189→
   190→    relation = fm.get("relation", "has")
   191→    negation = relation.upper() == "NOT" if relation else False
   192→
   193→    raw_unit = fm.get("unit")
   194→    _, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)
   195→
   196→    value_concept_id: int | None = None
   197→    entity_text = fm.get("entity")
   198→    raw_value = str(fm.get("value", ""))
   199→
   200→    # Ordinal-first: check if entity is an ordinal scale
   201→    ordinal_result = normalize_ordinal_value(raw_value, entity_text)
   202→    if ordinal_result is not None:
   203→        ordinal_value_cid, ordinal_unit_cid = ordinal_result
   204→        value_concept_id = ordinal_value_cid
   205→        if ordinal_unit_cid is not None:
   206→            unit_concept_id = ordinal_unit_cid
   207→    elif value_text and value_numeric is None:
   208→        _, value_concept_id = normalize_value(value_text)
   209→
   210→    return AtomicCriterion(
   211→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   212→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   213→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   214→        entity_concept_id=fm.get("entity_concept_id"),
   215→        entity_concept_system=fm.get("entity_concept_system"),
   216→        omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   217→        entity_domain=fm.get("entity_domain"),
   218→        relation_operator=relation,
   219→        value_numeric=value_numeric,
   220→        value_text=value_text,
   221→        unit_text=raw_unit,
   222→        unit_concept_id=unit_concept_id,
   223→        value_concept_id=value_concept_id,
   224→        negation=negation,
   225→        original_text=criterion_text,
   226→        confidence_score=fm.get("confidence_score"),
   227→    )
   228→
   229→
   230→def _build_tree_from_logic(
   231→    node: LogicNode,
   232→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   233→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion],
   234→    criterion_id: str,
   235→    protocol_id: str,
   236→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   237→    session: Session,
   238→) -> ExpressionNode:
   239→    """Recursively build ExpressionNode tree and DB records from LogicNode.
   240→
   241→    Creates CompositeCriterion and CriterionRelationship records for
   242→    branch nodes. Leaf nodes reference already-created AtomicCriterion
   243→    records by index.
   244→
   245→    Args:
   246→        node: Current LogicNode from LLM output.
   247→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts.
   248→        atomic_records: Pre-created AtomicCriterion records (indexed).
   249→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   250→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   251→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   252→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   253→
   254→    Returns:
   255→        ExpressionNode for JSONB storage.
   256→    """
   257→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   258→        idx = node.field_mapping_index or 0
   259→        atomic = atomic_records[idx]
   260→        fm = field_mappings[idx]
   261→        return ExpressionNode(
   262→            type="ATOMIC",
   263→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   264→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   265→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   266→            value=fm.get("value"),
   267→            unit=fm.get("unit"),
   268→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   269→        )
   270→
   271→    # Branch node: create CompositeCriterion
   272→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   273→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   274→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   275→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   276→        logic_operator=node.node_type,
   277→    )
   278→    session.add(composite)
   279→    session.flush()
   280→
   281→    children_nodes: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   282→    for seq, child in enumerate(node.children or []):
   283→        child_expr = _build_tree_from_logic(
   284→            child,
   285→            field_mappings,
   286→            atomic_records,
   287→            criterion_id,
   288→            protocol_id,
   289→            inclusion_exclusion,
   290→            session,
   291→        )
   292→        children_nodes.append(child_expr)
   293→
   294→        # Create relationship edge
   295→        if child.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   296→            child_id = child_expr.atomic_criterion_id or ""
   297→            child_type = "atomic"
   298→        else:
   299→            # For composite children, the CompositeCriterion was just
   300→            # created in the recursive call — we need its ID.
   301→            # We stored it in the ExpressionNode via a convention:
   302→            # non-ATOMIC nodes don't have atomic_criterion_id set.
   303→            # Query the last composite created for this criterion.
   304→            child_id = _find_last_composite_id(session, criterion_id, child.node_type)
   305→            child_type = "composite"
   306→
   307→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   308→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   309→            child_criterion_id=child_id,
   310→            child_type=child_type,
   311→            child_sequence=seq,
   312→        )
   313→        session.add(rel)
   314→
   315→    return ExpressionNode(
   316→        type=node.node_type,
   317→        children=children_nodes,
   318→    )
   319→
   320→
   321→def _find_last_composite_id(
   322→    session: Session, criterion_id: str, logic_operator: str
   323→) -> str:
   324→    """Find the most recently added composite criterion ID.
   325→
   326→    This is used during recursive tree building to link parent→child
   327→    composite relationships.
   328→
   329→    Args:
   330→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   331→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   332→        logic_operator: Logic operator of the child composite.
   333→
   334→    Returns:
   335→        ID of the most recent CompositeCriterion matching the criteria.
   336→    """
   337→    from sqlmodel import select
   338→
   339→    stmt = (
   340→        select(CompositeCriterion)
   341→        .where(CompositeCriterion.criterion_id == criterion_id)
   342→        .where(CompositeCriterion.logic_operator == logic_operator)
   343→        .order_by(CompositeCriterion.created_at.desc())  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
   344→    )
   345→    result = session.exec(stmt).first()
   346→    return result.id if result else ""
   347→
   348→
   349→async def build_expression_tree(
   350→    criterion_text: str,
   351→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   352→    criterion_id: str,
   353→    protocol_id: str,
   354→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   355→    session: Session,
   356→) -> StructuredCriterionTree:
   357→    """Build an expression tree from criterion field_mappings.
   358→
   359→    Two-pass approach:
   360→    1. LLM pass: call detect_logic_structure() for AND/OR/NOT detection
   361→    2. Fallback: if LLM fails or single mapping, AND-of-all-atomics
   362→
   363→    Creates AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping, plus
   364→    CompositeCriterion + CriterionRelationship for tree structure.
   365→
   366→    Args:
   367→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
   368→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
   369→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   370→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   371→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   372→        session: Active SQLModel session (records are added but not committed).
   373→
   374→    Returns:
   375→        StructuredCriterionTree for JSONB storage in criteria.structured_criterion.
   376→    """
   377→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   378→
   379→    # Create AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping
   380→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion] = []
   381→    for fm in field_mappings:
   382→        atomic = _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   383→            fm, criterion_id, protocol_id, inclusion_exclusion, criterion_text
   384→        )
   385→        session.add(atomic)
   386→        session.flush()
   387→        atomic_records.append(atomic)
   388→
   389→    # Pass 1: LLM logic detection
   390→    logic_response = await detect_logic_structure(criterion_text, field_mappings)
   391→
   392→    if logic_response is not None:
   393→        # Build tree from LLM-detected structure
   394→        root_expr = _build_tree_from_logic(
   395→            logic_response.root,
   396→            field_mappings,
   397→            atomic_records,
   398→            criterion_id,
   399→            protocol_id,
   400→            inclusion_exclusion,
   401→            session,
   402→        )
   403→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   404→            root=root_expr,
   405→            structure_confidence="llm",
   406→            structure_model=gemini_model_name,
   407→        )
   408→
   409→    # Pass 2: Fallback — AND-of-all-atomics (or single ATOMIC)
   410→    if len(atomic_records) == 1:
   411→        fm = field_mappings[0]
   412→        root_expr = ExpressionNode(
   413→            type="ATOMIC",
   414→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic_records[0].id,
   415→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   416→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   417→            value=fm.get("value"),
   418→            unit=fm.get("unit"),
   419→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   420→        )
   421→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   422→            root=root_expr,
   423→            structure_confidence="fallback",
   424→            structure_model=None,
   425→        )
   426→
   427→    # Multiple mappings, LLM failed → wrap in AND
   428→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   429→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   430→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   431→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   432→        logic_operator="AND",
   433→        original_text=criterion_text,
   434→    )
   435→    session.add(composite)
   436→    session.flush()
   437→
   438→    children: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   439→    for seq, (atomic, fm) in enumerate(zip(atomic_records, field_mappings)):
   440→        children.append(
   441→            ExpressionNode(
   442→                type="ATOMIC",
   443→                atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   444→                entity=fm.get("entity"),
   445→                relation=fm.get("relation"),
   446→                value=fm.get("value"),
   447→                unit=fm.get("unit"),
   448→                omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   449→            )
   450→        )
   451→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   452→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   453→            child_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   454→            child_type="atomic",
   455→            child_sequence=seq,
   456→        )
   457→        session.add(rel)
   458→
   459→    root_expr = ExpressionNode(type="AND", children=children)
   460→    return StructuredCriterionTree(
   461→        root=root_expr,
   462→        structure_confidence="fallback",
   463→        structure_model=None,
   464→    )
   465→
```

     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"
     6→Per user decision: "MedGemma and Gemini collaborate: Gemini uses MedGemma as
     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→import os
    17→from typing import Any
    18→
    19→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    20→
    21→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    22→
    23→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    24→
    25→
    26→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    27→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    28→
    29→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
    30→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
    31→    """
    32→
    33→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
    34→    relation: str = Field(
    35→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'has', 'is')"
    36→    )
    37→    value: str = Field(
    38→        description=(
    39→            "The specific value or threshold (e.g. '7%', 'positive', 'confirmed')"
    40→        )
    41→    )
    42→    unit: str | None = Field(
    43→        default=None,
    44→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
    45→    )
    46→
    47→
    48→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
    49→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
    50→
    51→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
    52→        default_factory=list,
    53→        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
    54→    )
    55→
    56→
    57→async def generate_field_mappings(
    58→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
    59→    criterion_text: str,
    60→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    61→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
    62→
    63→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
    64→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
    65→
    66→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
    67→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
    68→
    69→    Args:
    70→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
    71→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
    72→
    73→    Returns:
    74→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value, unit.
    75→        Empty list if generation fails.
    76→    """
    77→    if not criterion_text:
    78→        return []
    79→
    80→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
    81→    if not google_api_key:
    82→        logger.warning(
    83→            "GOOGLE_API_KEY not set — skipping field mapping generation for '%s'",
    84→            entity.entity_text[:50],
    85→        )
    86→        return []
    87→
    88→    try:
    89→        from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    90→
    91→        gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
    92→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    93→            model=gemini_model_name,
    94→            google_api_key=google_api_key,
    95→        )
    96→        structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(FieldMappingResponse)
    97→
    98→        # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
    99→        grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
   100→        code_context = ""
   101→        if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
   102→            system = entity.selected_system.upper()
   103→            code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
   104→
   105→        prompt = (
   106→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Decompose the"
   107→            " following criterion into structured AutoCriteria field"
   108→            " mappings using the Entity-Relation-Value-Unit pattern.\n\n"
   109→            f"Medical entity: {grounded_term} {code_context}\n"
   110→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   111→            "Instructions:\n"
   112→            "- Extract each discrete measurement, threshold, or condition"
   113→            " as a separate mapping\n"
   114→            "- entity: the specific measurement or concept"
   115→            " (e.g. 'HbA1c', 'Age', 'eGFR')\n"
   116→            "- relation: the logical operator"
   117→            " (<, >, >=, <=, =, has, is, within, not)\n"
   118→            "- value: the specific threshold or value"
   119→            " (e.g. '7', 'positive', 'confirmed')\n"
   120→            "- unit: the unit if applicable (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')"
   121→            " or null\n"
   122→            "- Create one mapping per discrete condition in the criterion\n"
   123→            "- If no clear measurement exists, create one mapping with"
   124→            " relation='has', value='confirmed'"
   125→        )
   126→
   127→        result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   128→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   129→            response = FieldMappingResponse.model_validate(result)
   130→        else:
   131→            response = result  # type: ignore[assignment]
   132→
   133→        mappings = [
   134→            {
   135→                "entity": m.entity,
   136→                "relation": m.relation,
   137→                "value": m.value,
   138→                "unit": m.unit,
   139→                "entity_concept_id": entity.selected_code,
   140→                "entity_concept_system": entity.selected_system,
   141→                "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   142→            }
   143→            for m in response.mappings
   144→        ]
   145→
   146→        logger.info(
   147→            "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   148→            len(mappings),
   149→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   150→        )
   151→        return mappings
   152→
   153→    except Exception as e:
   154→        logger.warning(
   155→            "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s': %s",
   156→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   157→            e,
   158→            exc_info=True,
   159→        )
   160→        # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   161→        return []
   162→
