```
     1→"""OMOP vocabulary mapper: look up standard OMOP concept_ids for medical entities.
     2→
     3→Queries Athena vocabulary tables (concept, concept_synonym) in a dedicated
     4→OMOP vocabulary database. Requires OMOP_VOCAB_URL environment variable
     5→pointing to a Postgres instance with loaded Athena vocabulary data.
     6→
     7→This tool provides the OMOP grounding leg of the dual-grounding pipeline:
     8→- TerminologyRouter provides UMLS/SNOMED/RxNorm/ICD-10/LOINC/HPO codes
     9→- OmopMapper provides OMOP standard concept_ids for CIRCE export and CDM joins
    10→
    11→Connection: Uses a dedicated SQLAlchemy engine created from OMOP_VOCAB_URL.
    12→This is separate from the main app database (DATABASE_URL). If OMOP_VOCAB_URL
    13→is not set, lookup_omop_concept raises RuntimeError — callers must handle this
    14→explicitly rather than silently receiving empty results.
    15→
    16→All DB I/O is synchronous SQLAlchemy wrapped in run_in_executor for async
    17→compatibility.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import asyncio
    23→import logging
    24→import os
    25→from difflib import SequenceMatcher
    26→from typing import Any
    27→
    28→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    29→from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
    30→
    31→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    32→
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→# Configuration
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→
    37→OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE: float = 0.3
    38→"""Minimum fuzzy-match score to accept a candidate. Below this, return None."""
    39→
    40→OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES: int = 50
    41→"""Maximum number of rows to fetch from each SQL query (LIMIT clause)."""
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Dedicated OMOP vocabulary engine (lazy singleton)
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→_omop_engine: Any = None
    48→
    49→
    50→def _get_omop_engine() -> Any:
    51→    """Return the OMOP vocabulary SQLAlchemy engine.
    52→
    53→    Creates a dedicated engine from OMOP_VOCAB_URL on first call.
    54→    Raises RuntimeError if the env var is not set — callers must
    55→    handle this rather than silently degrading.
    56→    """
    57→    global _omop_engine  # noqa: PLW0603
    58→    if _omop_engine is not None:
    59→        return _omop_engine
    60→
    61→    omop_url = os.getenv("OMOP_VOCAB_URL")
    62→    if not omop_url:
    63→        raise RuntimeError(
    64→            "OMOP_VOCAB_URL environment variable is not set. "
    65→            "Set it to the OMOP vocabulary Postgres connection string "
    66→            "(e.g. postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5433/omop_vocab) "
    67→            "or start the omop-vocab container: "
    68→            "docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml --profile omop up -d"
    69→        )
    70→
    71→    _omop_engine = create_engine(omop_url, pool_pre_ping=True)
    72→    logger.info("OMOP vocabulary engine created: %s", omop_url.split("@")[-1])
    73→    return _omop_engine
    74→
    75→
    76→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    77→# Entity type -> OMOP domain mapping
    78→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    79→
    80→ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN: dict[str, str] = {
    81→    "Condition": "Condition",
    82→    "Medication": "Drug",
    83→    "Lab_Value": "Measurement",
    84→    "Procedure": "Procedure",
    85→    "Demographic": "Observation",
    86→}
    87→"""Map pipeline entity types to OMOP CDM domain_id values.
    88→
    89→Demographic is a catch-all routed to Observation. Entity types not present
    90→here will fall back to Observation as well.
    91→"""
    92→
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→# Result schema
    95→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    96→
    97→
    98→class OmopLookupResult(BaseModel):
    99→    """Result of an OMOP vocabulary lookup for a single entity.
   100→
   101→    Attributes:
   102→        omop_concept_id: Matched OMOP concept_id as a string, or None.
   103→        omop_concept_name: Matched concept_name from the vocabulary, or None.
   104→        omop_vocabulary_id: Vocabulary source of the match (e.g. SNOMED, RxNorm).
   105→        omop_domain_id: OMOP domain_id of the matched concept.
   106→        match_score: Fuzzy similarity score between 0.0 and 1.0.
   107→        match_method: How the match was found: "concept_name" or "synonym".
   108→    """
   109→
   110→    omop_concept_id: str | None = Field(
   111→        default=None,
   112→        description=("Matched OMOP concept_id as a string, or None if no match."),
   113→    )
   114→    omop_concept_name: str | None = Field(
   115→        default=None,
   116→        description="Matched concept_name from the OMOP vocabulary.",
   117→    )
   118→    omop_vocabulary_id: str | None = Field(
   119→        default=None,
   120→        description=("Vocabulary source of the match (e.g. 'SNOMED', 'RxNorm')."),
   121→    )
   122→    omop_domain_id: str | None = Field(
   123→        default=None,
   124→        description="OMOP domain_id of the matched concept.",
   125→    )
   126→    match_score: float = Field(
   127→        default=0.0,
   128→        ge=0.0,
   129→        le=1.0,
   130→        description="Fuzzy similarity score between 0.0 and 1.0.",
   131→    )
   132→    match_method: str = Field(
   133→        default="",
   134→        description='How the match was found: "concept_name" or "synonym".',
   135→    )
   136→
   137→
   138→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   139→# Internal helpers
   140→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   141→
   142→
   143→def _score_candidates(
   144→    entity_text: str, candidates: list[dict[str, Any]]
   145→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   146→    """Score and sort candidates by fuzzy string similarity to *entity_text*.
   147→
   148→    Uses ``difflib.SequenceMatcher`` ratio as the base score, with a bonus
   149→    for exact case-insensitive substring containment.
   150→
   151→    Args:
   152→        entity_text: The original entity text to match against.
   153→        candidates: List of candidate dicts, each with at least a
   154→            ``"match_text"`` key containing the string to compare.
   155→
   156→    Returns:
   157→        The same candidate dicts augmented with a ``"score"`` key, sorted
   158→        in descending order of score.
   159→    """
   160→    entity_lower = entity_text.lower().strip()
   161→
   162→    for candidate in candidates:
   163→        match_text = candidate.get("match_text", "").lower().strip()
   164→
   165→        # Base score: SequenceMatcher ratio
   166→        base_score = SequenceMatcher(None, entity_lower, match_text).ratio()
   167→
   168→        # Bonus for exact substring containment (either direction)
   169→        bonus = 0.0
   170→        if entity_lower in match_text or match_text in entity_lower:
   171→            bonus = 0.15
   172→
   173→        # Bonus for exact match
   174→        if entity_lower == match_text:
   175→            bonus = 0.25
   176→
   177→        candidate["score"] = min(base_score + bonus, 1.0)
   178→
   179→    # Sort by score desc, then by length similarity (prefer match_text
   180→    # closest in length to entity_text) as tiebreaker.
   181→    entity_len = len(entity_lower)
   182→
   183→    def _sort_key(c: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[float, int]:
   184→        match_len = len(c.get("match_text", "").strip())
   185→        return (c["score"], -abs(match_len - entity_len))
   186→
   187→    candidates.sort(key=_sort_key, reverse=True)
   188→    return candidates
   189→
   190→
   191→def _get_domain_filter(entity_type: str) -> str:
   192→    """Resolve entity type to an OMOP domain_id filter value.
   193→
   194→    Falls back to ``"Observation"`` for unknown entity types.
   195→
   196→    Args:
   197→        entity_type: Pipeline entity type (e.g. "Condition", "Medication").
   198→
   199→    Returns:
   200→        OMOP domain_id string.
   201→    """
   202→    domain = ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN.get(entity_type, "Observation")
   203→    if entity_type not in ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN:
   204→        logger.info(
   205→            "Entity type '%s' not in OMOP domain map — defaulting to '%s'",
   206→            entity_type,
   207→            domain,
   208→        )
   209→    return domain
   210→
   211→
   212→def _query_concept_table(
   213→    engine: Any, entity_text: str, domain_id: str
   214→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   215→    """Query concept table for standard concepts matching *entity_text*.
   216→
   217→    Args:
   218→        engine: SQLAlchemy engine for the OMOP vocabulary database.
   219→        entity_text: Text to search for (used in ILIKE pattern).
   220→        domain_id: OMOP domain_id filter.
   221→
   222→    Returns:
   223→        List of candidate dicts with keys: concept_id, concept_name,
   224→        domain_id, vocabulary_id, match_text, match_method.
   225→    """
   226→    sql = text(
   227→        "SELECT concept_id, concept_name, domain_id, vocabulary_id "
   228→        "FROM concept "
   229→        "WHERE standard_concept = 'S' "
   230→        "  AND domain_id = :domain_id "
   231→        "  AND concept_name ILIKE :pattern "
   232→        "ORDER BY length(concept_name) ASC "
   233→        "LIMIT :max_candidates"
   234→    )
   235→    pattern = f"%{entity_text}%"
   236→    with engine.connect() as conn:
   237→        rows = conn.execute(
   238→            sql,
   239→            {
   240→                "domain_id": domain_id,
   241→                "pattern": pattern,
   242→                "max_candidates": OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES,
   243→            },
   244→        ).fetchall()
   245→
   246→    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   247→    for row in rows:
   248→        candidates.append(
   249→            {
   250→                "concept_id": str(row[0]),
   251→                "concept_name": row[1],
   252→                "domain_id": row[2],
   253→                "vocabulary_id": row[3],
   254→                "match_text": row[1],
   255→                "match_method": "concept_name",
   256→            }
   257→        )
   258→    return candidates
   259→
   260→
   261→def _query_synonym_table(
   262→    engine: Any, entity_text: str, domain_id: str
   263→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   264→    """Query concept_synonym joined with concept for synonym matches.
   265→
   266→    Args:
   267→        engine: SQLAlchemy engine for the OMOP vocabulary database.
   268→        entity_text: Text to search for (used in ILIKE pattern).
   269→        domain_id: OMOP domain_id filter.
   270→
   271→    Returns:
   272→        List of candidate dicts with keys: concept_id, concept_name,
   273→        domain_id, vocabulary_id, match_text, match_method.
   274→    """
   275→    sql = text(
   276→        "SELECT c.concept_id, c.concept_name, c.domain_id, "
   277→        "       c.vocabulary_id, s.concept_synonym_name "
   278→        "FROM concept_synonym s "
   279→        "JOIN concept c ON s.concept_id = c.concept_id "
   280→        "WHERE c.standard_concept = 'S' "
   281→        "  AND c.domain_id = :domain_id "
   282→        "  AND s.concept_synonym_name ILIKE :pattern "
   283→        "ORDER BY length(s.concept_synonym_name) ASC "
   284→        "LIMIT :max_candidates"
   285→    )
   286→    pattern = f"%{entity_text}%"
   287→    with engine.connect() as conn:
   288→        rows = conn.execute(
   289→            sql,
   290→            {
   291→                "domain_id": domain_id,
   292→                "pattern": pattern,
   293→                "max_candidates": OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES,
   294→            },
   295→        ).fetchall()
   296→
   297→    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   298→    for row in rows:
   299→        candidates.append(
   300→            {
   301→                "concept_id": str(row[0]),
   302→                "concept_name": row[1],
   303→                "domain_id": row[2],
   304→                "vocabulary_id": row[3],
   305→                "match_text": row[4],  # synonym name used for scoring
   306→                "match_method": "synonym",
   307→            }
   308→        )
   309→    return candidates
   310→
   311→
   312→def _sync_lookup(entity_text: str, domain_id: str) -> OmopLookupResult:
   313→    """Synchronous OMOP lookup — runs inside run_in_executor.
   314→
   315→    Queries both concept and concept_synonym tables, deduplicates
   316→    candidates by concept_id (preferring concept_name match), scores them,
   317→    and returns the best match above the threshold.
   318→
   319→    Args:
   320→        entity_text: The entity text to look up.
   321→        domain_id: OMOP domain_id to filter on.
   322→
   323→    Returns:
   324→        OmopLookupResult with best match, or empty result if nothing found
   325→        or all candidates score below OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE.
   326→
   327→    Raises:
   328→        RuntimeError: If OMOP_VOCAB_URL is not configured.
   329→        Exception: Database errors are propagated, not swallowed.
   330→    """
   331→    engine = _get_omop_engine()
   332→
   333→    concept_candidates = _query_concept_table(engine, entity_text, domain_id)
   334→    synonym_candidates = _query_synonym_table(engine, entity_text, domain_id)
   335→
   336→    # Deduplicate by concept_id — prefer concept_name match over synonym
   337→    seen: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
   338→    for candidate in concept_candidates:
   339→        seen[candidate["concept_id"]] = candidate
   340→    for candidate in synonym_candidates:
   341→        if candidate["concept_id"] not in seen:
   342→            seen[candidate["concept_id"]] = candidate
   343→
   344→    all_candidates = list(seen.values())
   345→
   346→    if not all_candidates:
   347→        logger.debug(
   348→            "No OMOP candidates found for '%s' in domain '%s'",
   349→            entity_text[:50],
   350→            domain_id,
   351→        )
   352→        return OmopLookupResult()
   353→
   354→    # Score and sort
   355→    scored = _score_candidates(entity_text, all_candidates)
   356→    best = scored[0]
   357→
   358→    if best["score"] < OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE:
   359→        logger.debug(
   360→            "Best OMOP candidate for '%s' scored %.3f "
   361→            "(below threshold %.2f) — skipping",
   362→            entity_text[:50],
   363→            best["score"],
   364→            OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE,
   365→        )
   366→        return OmopLookupResult()
   367→
   368→    logger.info(
   369→        "OMOP match for '%s': concept_id=%s, name='%s', score=%.3f, method=%s",
   370→        entity_text[:50],
   371→        best["concept_id"],
   372→        best["concept_name"][:60],
   373→        best["score"],
   374→        best["match_method"],
   375→    )
   376→
   377→    return OmopLookupResult(
   378→        omop_concept_id=best["concept_id"],
   379→        omop_concept_name=best["concept_name"],
   380→        omop_vocabulary_id=best["vocabulary_id"],
   381→        omop_domain_id=best["domain_id"],
   382→        match_score=best["score"],
   383→        match_method=best["match_method"],
   384→    )
   385→
   386→
   387→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   388→# Public async API
   389→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   390→
   391→
   392→async def lookup_omop_concept(entity_text: str, entity_type: str) -> OmopLookupResult:
   393→    """Look up the best OMOP standard concept for a medical entity.
   394→
   395→    Maps the entity type to an OMOP domain filter, queries the concept
   396→    and concept_synonym tables in the OMOP vocabulary database, scores
   397→    candidates with fuzzy string matching, and returns the best result
   398→    above the minimum similarity threshold.
   399→
   400→    The synchronous database I/O is executed in a thread-pool executor to
   401→    avoid blocking the async event loop.
   402→
   403→    Requires OMOP_VOCAB_URL to be set. Raises RuntimeError if not configured.
   404→
   405→    Args:
   406→        entity_text: The medical entity text to look up (e.g. "metformin",
   407→            "type 2 diabetes mellitus", "hemoglobin A1c").
   408→        entity_type: Pipeline entity type used to determine the OMOP domain
   409→            filter. One of "Condition", "Medication", "Lab_Value",
   410→            "Procedure", "Demographic". Unknown types fall back to
   411→            "Observation".
   412→
   413→    Returns:
   414→        OmopLookupResult with the best-matching OMOP concept, or an empty
   415→        result if no match is found above the similarity threshold.
   416→
   417→    Raises:
   418→        RuntimeError: If OMOP_VOCAB_URL is not set.
   419→    """
   420→    if not entity_text or not entity_text.strip():
   421→        raise ValueError("Empty entity_text passed to lookup_omop_concept")
   422→
   423→    domain_id = _get_domain_filter(entity_type)
   424→
   425→    logger.debug(
   426→        "OMOP lookup: entity='%s', type='%s', domain='%s'",
   427→        entity_text[:50],
   428→        entity_type,
   429→        domain_id,
   430→    )
   431→
   432→    loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   433→    result = await loop.run_in_executor(None, _sync_lookup, entity_text, domain_id)
   434→
   435→    return result
   436→
```

```
     1→"""OHDSI CIRCE CohortExpression JSON builder.
     2→
     3→Converts structured criteria (expression trees with atomic/composite nodes)
     4→into the CIRCE JSON format consumed by OHDSI Atlas and WebAPI.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from typing import Any
    10→
    11→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion
    12→
    13→from api_service.exporters import ProtocolExportData
    14→
    15→# Map our entity_domain values to CIRCE domain table names
    16→_DOMAIN_TO_CIRCE: dict[str, str] = {
    17→    "condition": "ConditionOccurrence",
    18→    "measurement": "Measurement",
    19→    "drug": "DrugExposure",
    20→    "procedure": "ProcedureOccurrence",
    21→    "observation": "Observation",
    22→    "device": "DeviceExposure",
    23→    "visit": "VisitOccurrence",
    24→}
    25→
    26→# Map relation_operator to CIRCE Op string
    27→_OP_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    28→    ">": "gt",
    29→    ">=": "gte",
    30→    "<": "lt",
    31→    "<=": "lte",
    32→    "=": "eq",
    33→    "==": "eq",
    34→    "!=": "neq",
    35→    "gt": "gt",
    36→    "gte": "gte",
    37→    "lt": "lt",
    38→    "lte": "lte",
    39→    "eq": "eq",
    40→    "neq": "neq",
    41→}
    42→
    43→
    44→def build_circe_export(data: ProtocolExportData) -> dict[str, Any]:
    45→    """Build a CIRCE CohortExpression JSON from structured criteria.
    46→
    47→    Args:
    48→        data: Loaded protocol export data.
    49→
    50→    Returns:
    51→        Dict representing the CIRCE CohortExpression.
    52→    """
    53→    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    54→    concept_set_index: dict[str, int] = {}  # concept_id -> index in concept_sets
    55→
    56→    inclusion_criteria: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    57→    censoring_criteria: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    58→
    59→    for criterion in data.criteria:
    60→        tree = criterion.structured_criterion
    61→        if not tree:
    62→            continue
    63→
    64→        is_exclusion = criterion.criteria_type == "exclusion"
    65→        group = _build_criteria_group(tree, data, concept_sets, concept_set_index)
    66→        if group is None:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        if is_exclusion:
    70→            censoring_criteria.append(group)
    71→        else:
    72→            inclusion_criteria.append(group)
    73→
    74→    expression: dict[str, Any] = {
    75→        "ConceptSets": concept_sets,
    76→        "PrimaryCriteria": {
    77→            "CriteriaList": [],
    78→            "ObservationWindow": {
    79→                "PriorDays": 0,
    80→                "PostDays": 0,
    81→            },
    82→            "PrimaryCriteriaLimit": {"Type": "First"},
    83→        },
    84→        "AdditionalCriteria": {
    85→            "Type": "ALL",
    86→            "CriteriaList": inclusion_criteria,
    87→        },
    88→        "CensoringCriteria": censoring_criteria,
    89→    }
    90→
    91→    return expression
    92→
    93→
    94→def _get_concept_id(atomic: AtomicCriterion) -> int | None:
    95→    """Get the best concept ID for an atomic criterion.
    96→
    97→    Prefers omop_concept_id, falls back to entity_concept_id.
    98→    Returns None if neither is a valid integer.
    99→    """
   100→    for field_val in (atomic.omop_concept_id, atomic.entity_concept_id):
   101→        if field_val is not None:
   102→            try:
   103→                return int(field_val)
   104→            except (ValueError, TypeError):
   105→                continue
   106→    return None
   107→
   108→
   109→def _ensure_concept_set(
   110→    atomic: AtomicCriterion,
   111→    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]],
   112→    concept_set_index: dict[str, int],
   113→) -> int | None:
   114→    """Ensure a ConceptSet exists for the atomic's concept ID.
   115→
   116→    Returns the ConceptSet index, or None if no valid concept ID.
   117→    """
   118→    concept_id = _get_concept_id(atomic)
   119→    if concept_id is None:
   120→        return None
   121→
   122→    key = str(concept_id)
   123→    if key in concept_set_index:
   124→        return concept_set_index[key]
   125→
   126→    idx = len(concept_sets)
   127→    concept_sets.append(
   128→        {
   129→            "id": idx,
   130→            "name": atomic.original_text or f"Concept {concept_id}",
   131→            "expression": {
   132→                "items": [
   133→                    {
   134→                        "concept": {
   135→                            "CONCEPT_ID": concept_id,
   136→                            "CONCEPT_NAME": atomic.original_text or "",
   137→                        },
   138→                        "isExcluded": False,
   139→                        "includeDescendants": True,
   140→                        "includeMapped": False,
   141→                    }
   142→                ],
   143→            },
   144→        }
   145→    )
   146→    concept_set_index[key] = idx
   147→    return idx
   148→
   149→
   150→def _build_atomic_criteria(
   151→    atomic: AtomicCriterion,
   152→    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]],
   153→    concept_set_index: dict[str, int],
   154→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   155→    """Build a CIRCE criteria entry from an AtomicCriterion."""
   156→    cs_idx = _ensure_concept_set(atomic, concept_sets, concept_set_index)
   157→    if cs_idx is None:
   158→        return None
   159→
   160→    domain = _DOMAIN_TO_CIRCE.get(
   161→        (atomic.entity_domain or "").lower(), "ConditionOccurrence"
   162→    )
   163→
   164→    criteria_entry: dict[str, Any] = {
   165→        "Criteria": {
   166→            domain: {
   167→                "CodesetId": cs_idx,
   168→            },
   169→        },
   170→    }
   171→
   172→    # Handle negation: absence = OccurrenceCount of 0
   173→    if atomic.negation:
   174→        criteria_entry["Criteria"]["OccurrenceCount"] = {
   175→            "Value": 0,
   176→            "Op": "eq",
   177→        }
   178→
   179→    # Add value filter for measurements
   180→    if atomic.relation_operator and atomic.value_numeric is not None:
   181→        op_str = _OP_MAP.get(atomic.relation_operator, "eq")
   182→        value_filter: dict[str, Any] = {
   183→            "Value": atomic.value_numeric,
   184→            "Op": op_str,
   185→        }
   186→        if atomic.unit_concept_id is not None:
   187→            value_filter["UnitConceptId"] = atomic.unit_concept_id
   188→        criteria_entry["Criteria"]["ValueAsNumber"] = value_filter
   189→
   190→    return criteria_entry
   191→
   192→
   193→def _build_and_or_group(
   194→    tree: dict[str, Any],
   195→    data: ProtocolExportData,
   196→    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]],
   197→    concept_set_index: dict[str, int],
   198→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   199→    """Build a CIRCE AND/OR CriteriaGroup from child nodes."""
   200→    node_type = tree.get("type", "").upper()
   201→    children = tree.get("children", [])
   202→    child_criteria = []
   203→    for child in children:
   204→        result = _build_criteria_group(child, data, concept_sets, concept_set_index)
   205→        if result is not None:
   206→            child_criteria.append(result)
   207→
   208→    if not child_criteria:
   209→        return None
   210→
   211→    group_type = "ALL" if node_type == "AND" else "ANY"
   212→    return {"Type": group_type, "CriteriaList": child_criteria}
   213→
   214→
   215→def _build_not_group(
   216→    tree: dict[str, Any],
   217→    data: ProtocolExportData,
   218→    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]],
   219→    concept_set_index: dict[str, int],
   220→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   221→    """Build a negated CIRCE criteria entry."""
   222→    children = tree.get("children", [])
   223→    if not children:
   224→        return None
   225→    inner = _build_criteria_group(children[0], data, concept_sets, concept_set_index)
   226→    if inner is None:
   227→        return None
   228→    if "Criteria" in inner:
   229→        inner["Criteria"]["OccurrenceCount"] = {
   230→            "Value": 0,
   231→            "Op": "eq",
   232→        }
   233→    return inner
   234→
   235→
   236→def _build_criteria_group(
   237→    tree: dict[str, Any],
   238→    data: ProtocolExportData,
   239→    concept_sets: list[dict[str, Any]],
   240→    concept_set_index: dict[str, int],
   241→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   242→    """Recursively build a CIRCE CriteriaGroup from an expression tree."""
   243→    node_type = tree.get("type", "").upper()
   244→
   245→    if node_type == "ATOMIC":
   246→        atomic_id = tree.get("atomic_criterion_id")
   247→        if not atomic_id:
   248→            return None
   249→        atomic = data.atomics_by_id.get(atomic_id)
   250→        if not atomic:
   251→            return None
   252→        return _build_atomic_criteria(atomic, concept_sets, concept_set_index)
   253→
   254→    if node_type in ("AND", "OR"):
   255→        return _build_and_or_group(tree, data, concept_sets, concept_set_index)
   256→
   257→    if node_type == "NOT":
   258→        return _build_not_group(tree, data, concept_sets, concept_set_index)
   259→
   260→    return None
   261→
```

```
     1→"""FHIR R4 Group resource builder (EBM IG).
     2→
     3→Converts structured criteria into a FHIR Group resource with
     4→characteristics representing eligibility criteria.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from typing import Any
    10→from uuid import uuid4
    11→
    12→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion
    13→
    14→from api_service.exporters import ProtocolExportData
    15→
    16→# Map entity_concept_system to FHIR system URIs
    17→_SYSTEM_URI_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    18→    "snomed": "http://snomed.info/sct",
    19→    "snomedct": "http://snomed.info/sct",
    20→    "loinc": "http://loinc.org",
    21→    "rxnorm": "http://www.nlm.nih.gov/research/umls/rxnorm",
    22→    "icd10": "http://hl7.org/fhir/sid/icd-10",
    23→    "icd10cm": "http://hl7.org/fhir/sid/icd-10-cm",
    24→    "cpt": "http://www.ama-assn.org/go/cpt",
    25→    "hcpcs": "urn:oid:2.16.840.1.113883.6.285",
    26→    "omop": "http://ohdsi.org/omop/concept",
    27→}
    28→
    29→# Map our relation_operator to FHIR comparator
    30→_COMPARATOR_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    31→    ">": "gt",
    32→    ">=": "ge",
    33→    "<": "lt",
    34→    "<=": "le",
    35→    "gt": "gt",
    36→    "gte": "ge",
    37→    "lt": "lt",
    38→    "lte": "le",
    39→}
    40→
    41→
    42→def build_fhir_group_export(data: ProtocolExportData) -> dict[str, Any]:
    43→    """Build a FHIR R4 Group resource from structured criteria.
    44→
    45→    Args:
    46→        data: Loaded protocol export data.
    47→
    48→    Returns:
    49→        Dict representing the FHIR Group resource.
    50→    """
    51→    characteristics: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    52→
    53→    for criterion in data.criteria:
    54→        tree = criterion.structured_criterion
    55→        if not tree:
    56→            continue
    57→
    58→        is_exclusion = criterion.criteria_type == "exclusion"
    59→        chars = _build_characteristics_from_tree(tree, data, exclude=is_exclusion)
    60→        characteristics.extend(chars)
    61→
    62→    group: dict[str, Any] = {
    63→        "resourceType": "Group",
    64→        "id": data.protocol.id,
    65→        "type": "person",
    66→        "actual": False,
    67→        "name": data.protocol.title,
    68→        "characteristic": characteristics,
    69→    }
    70→
    71→    # Set combination method if we have characteristics
    72→    if characteristics:
    73→        group["extension"] = [
    74→            {
    75→                "url": (
    76→                    "http://hl7.org/fhir/uv/ebm/StructureDefinition"
    77→                    "/characteristic-combination"
    78→                ),
    79→                "valueCode": "all-of",
    80→            }
    81→        ]
    82→
    83→    return group
    84→
    85→
    86→def _build_or_characteristic(
    87→    tree: dict[str, Any],
    88→    data: ProtocolExportData,
    89→    exclude: bool,
    90→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    91→    """Build a nested FHIR Group for OR logic."""
    92→    children = tree.get("children", [])
    93→    or_chars: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    94→    for child in children:
    95→        or_chars.extend(_build_characteristics_from_tree(child, data, exclude=exclude))
    96→    if not or_chars:
    97→        return []
    98→
    99→    nested_group_id = str(uuid4())
   100→    nested_group: dict[str, Any] = {
   101→        "resourceType": "Group",
   102→        "id": nested_group_id,
   103→        "type": "person",
   104→        "actual": False,
   105→        "characteristic": or_chars,
   106→        "extension": [
   107→            {
   108→                "url": (
   109→                    "http://hl7.org/fhir/uv/ebm/StructureDefinition"
   110→                    "/characteristic-combination"
   111→                ),
   112→                "valueCode": "any-of",
   113→            }
   114→        ],
   115→    }
   116→    return [
   117→        {
   118→            "code": {"text": "Nested OR group"},
   119→            "valueReference": {"reference": f"#/{nested_group_id}"},
   120→            "exclude": exclude,
   121→            "_contained": nested_group,
   122→        }
   123→    ]
   124→
   125→
   126→def _build_characteristics_from_tree(
   127→    tree: dict[str, Any],
   128→    data: ProtocolExportData,
   129→    exclude: bool = False,
   130→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   131→    """Recursively build FHIR characteristics from an expression tree."""
   132→    node_type = tree.get("type", "").upper()
   133→
   134→    if node_type == "ATOMIC":
   135→        atomic_id = tree.get("atomic_criterion_id")
   136→        atomic = data.atomics_by_id.get(atomic_id) if atomic_id else None
   137→        if not atomic:
   138→            return []
   139→        char = _build_characteristic(atomic, exclude=exclude)
   140→        return [char] if char else []
   141→
   142→    if node_type == "AND":
   143→        result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   144→        for child in tree.get("children", []):
   145→            result.extend(
   146→                _build_characteristics_from_tree(child, data, exclude=exclude)
   147→            )
   148→        return result
   149→
   150→    if node_type == "OR":
   151→        return _build_or_characteristic(tree, data, exclude)
   152→
   153→    if node_type == "NOT":
   154→        children = tree.get("children", [])
   155→        if not children:
   156→            return []
   157→        return _build_characteristics_from_tree(children[0], data, exclude=not exclude)
   158→
   159→    return []
   160→
   161→
   162→def _get_system_uri(system: str | None) -> str:
   163→    """Map an entity_concept_system to a FHIR system URI."""
   164→    if not system:
   165→        return "http://ohdsi.org/omop/concept"
   166→    return _SYSTEM_URI_MAP.get(system.lower(), f"urn:oid:{system}")
   167→
   168→
   169→def _get_concept_code(atomic: AtomicCriterion) -> str | None:
   170→    """Get the best concept code for an atomic criterion."""
   171→    for val in (atomic.omop_concept_id, atomic.entity_concept_id):
   172→        if val is not None:
   173→            return str(val)
   174→    return None
   175→
   176→
   177→def _build_characteristic(
   178→    atomic: AtomicCriterion,
   179→    exclude: bool = False,
   180→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   181→    """Build a single FHIR Group characteristic from an AtomicCriterion."""
   182→    concept_code = _get_concept_code(atomic)
   183→    if concept_code is None:
   184→        return None
   185→
   186→    system_uri = _get_system_uri(atomic.entity_concept_system)
   187→
   188→    char: dict[str, Any] = {
   189→        "code": {
   190→            "coding": [
   191→                {
   192→                    "system": system_uri,
   193→                    "code": concept_code,
   194→                    "display": atomic.original_text or "",
   195→                }
   196→            ],
   197→        },
   198→        "exclude": exclude or atomic.negation,
   199→    }
   200→
   201→    # Add value: quantity or text
   202→    if atomic.value_numeric is not None:
   203→        quantity: dict[str, Any] = {
   204→            "value": atomic.value_numeric,
   205→        }
   206→        if atomic.unit_text:
   207→            quantity["unit"] = atomic.unit_text
   208→        if atomic.relation_operator:
   209→            comparator = _COMPARATOR_MAP.get(atomic.relation_operator)
   210→            if comparator:
   211→                quantity["comparator"] = comparator
   212→        char["valueQuantity"] = quantity
   213→    elif atomic.value_text:
   214→        char["valueCodeableConcept"] = {
   215→            "text": atomic.value_text,
   216→        }
   217→    else:
   218→        # Boolean presence/absence
   219→        char["valueBoolean"] = not atomic.negation
   220→
   221→    return char
   222→
```

The matcher derives age from birthDate.\n    \"\"\"\n    if atomic.value_numeric is None:\n        return None\n\n    quantity: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"value\": atomic.value_numeric,\n        \"unit\": atomic.unit_text or \"years\",\n        \"system\": \"http://unitsofmeasure.org\",\n        \"code\": \"a\",\n    }\n    if atomic.relation_operator:\n        comparator = _COMPARATOR_MAP.get(atomic.relation_operator)\n        if comparator:\n            quantity[\"comparator\"] = comparator\n\n    return {\n        \"code\": {\n            \"coding\": [\n                {\n                    \"system\": (\n                        \"http://terminology.hl7.org/CodeSystem\"\n                        \"/usage-context-type\"\n                    ),\n                    \"code\": \"age\",\n                    \"display\": \"Age Range\",\n                }\n            ],\n        },\n        \"exclude\": exclude,\n        \"valueQuantity\": quantity,\n    }\n\n\ndef _build_characteristic(\n    atomic: AtomicCriterion,\n    exclude: bool = False,\n) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Build a single FHIR Group characteristic from an AtomicCriterion.\"\"\"\n    # Demographics: use age context type (not a coded concept)\n    if (atomic.entity_domain or \"\").lower() == \"demographics\":\n        return _build_demographic_characteristic(atomic, exclude=exclude)\n\n    concept_code = _get_concept_code(atomic)\n    if concept_code is None:\n        return None\n\n    system_uri = _get_system_uri(atomic.entity_concept_system)\n\n    char: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"code\": {\n            \"coding\": [\n                {\n                    \"system\": system_uri,\n                    \"code\": concept_code,\n                    \"display\": atomic.original_text or \"\",\n                }\n            ],\n        },\n        \"exclude\": exclude or atomic.negation,\n    }\n\n    # Add value: quantity or text\n    if atomic.value_numeric is not None:\n        quantity: dict[str, Any] = {\n            \"value\": atomic.value_numeric,\n        }\n        if atomic.unit_text:\n            quantity[\"unit\"] = atomic.unit_text\n        if atomic.relation_operator:\n            comparator = _COMPARATOR_MAP.get(atomic.relation_operator)\n            if comparator:\n                quantity[\"comparator\"] = comparator\n        char[\"valueQuantity\"] = quantity\n    elif atomic.value_text:\n        char[\"valueCodeableConcept\"] = {\n            \"text\": atomic.value_text,\n        }\n    else:\n        #      1→"""Tests for protocol export endpoints and builder functions.
```
     2→
     3→Covers CIRCE, FHIR Group, and evaluation SQL export builders (unit)
     4→and their FastAPI endpoints (integration).
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from uuid import uuid4
     8→
     9→from shared.models import (
    10→    AtomicCriterion,
    11→    CompositeCriterion,
    12→    Criteria,
    13→    CriteriaBatch,
    14→    CriterionRelationship,
    15→    Protocol,
    16→)
    17→
    18→from api_service.exporters import ProtocolExportData
    19→from api_service.exporters.circe_builder import build_circe_export
    20→from api_service.exporters.evaluation_sql_builder import build_evaluation_sql
    21→from api_service.exporters.fhir_group_builder import build_fhir_group_export
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Fixtures
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→
    28→def _make_protocol(db_session, **overrides):
    29→    """Create a test Protocol."""
    30→    defaults = {
    31→        "id": str(uuid4()),
    32→        "title": "Test Protocol",
    33→        "file_uri": "local://test.pdf",
    34→        "status": "complete",
    35→    }
    36→    defaults.update(overrides)
    37→    p = Protocol(**defaults)
    38→    db_session.add(p)
    39→    db_session.flush()
    40→    return p
    41→
    42→
    43→def _make_batch(db_session, protocol_id, **overrides):
    44→    """Create a test CriteriaBatch."""
    45→    defaults = {
    46→        "id": str(uuid4()),
    47→        "protocol_id": protocol_id,
    48→        "status": "pending_review",
    49→        "is_archived": False,
    50→    }
    51→    defaults.update(overrides)
    52→    b = CriteriaBatch(**defaults)
    53→    db_session.add(b)
    54→    db_session.flush()
    55→    return b
    56→
    57→
    58→def _make_criterion(db_session, batch_id, **overrides):
    59→    """Create a test Criteria record."""
    60→    defaults = {
    61→        "id": str(uuid4()),
    62→        "batch_id": batch_id,
    63→        "criteria_type": "inclusion",
    64→        "text": "HbA1c >= 7%",
    65→    }
    66→    defaults.update(overrides)
    67→    c = Criteria(**defaults)
    68→    db_session.add(c)
    69→    db_session.flush()
    70→    return c
    71→
    72→
    73→def _make_atomic(db_session, criterion_id, protocol_id, **overrides):
    74→    """Create a test AtomicCriterion."""
    75→    defaults = {
    76→        "id": str(uuid4()),
    77→        "criterion_id": criterion_id,
    78→        "protocol_id": protocol_id,
    79→        "inclusion_exclusion": "inclusion",
    80→        "entity_concept_id": "4532345",
    81→        "entity_concept_system": "snomed",
    82→        "omop_concept_id": "3004410",
    83→        "entity_domain": "measurement",
    84→        "relation_operator": ">=",
    85→        "value_numeric": 7.0,
    86→        "unit_text": "%",
    87→        "unit_concept_id": 8554,
    88→        "original_text": "HbA1c >= 7%",
    89→        "negation": False,
    90→    }
    91→    defaults.update(overrides)
    92→    a = AtomicCriterion(**defaults)
    93→    db_session.add(a)
    94→    db_session.flush()
    95→    return a
    96→
    97→
    98→def _make_composite(db_session, criterion_id, protocol_id, **overrides):
    99→    """Create a test CompositeCriterion."""
   100→    defaults = {
   101→        "id": str(uuid4()),
   102→        "criterion_id": criterion_id,
   103→        "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   104→        "inclusion_exclusion": "inclusion",
   105→        "logic_operator": "AND",
   106→    }
   107→    defaults.update(overrides)
   108→    c = CompositeCriterion(**defaults)
   109→    db_session.add(c)
   110→    db_session.flush()
   111→    return c
   112→
   113→
   114→def _make_relationship(db_session, parent_id, child_id, child_type, seq=0):
   115→    """Create a test CriterionRelationship."""
   116→    r = CriterionRelationship(
   117→        parent_criterion_id=parent_id,
   118→        child_criterion_id=child_id,
   119→        child_type=child_type,
   120→        child_sequence=seq,
   121→    )
   122→    db_session.add(r)
   123→    db_session.flush()
   124→    return r
   125→
   126→
   127→def _build_simple_export_data(
   128→    db_session,
   129→    criteria_type="inclusion",
   130→    negation=False,
   131→    entity_domain="measurement",
   132→    with_tree=True,
   133→):
   134→    """Build a simple ProtocolExportData with one criterion and one atomic."""
   135→    proto = _make_protocol(db_session)
   136→    batch = _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)
   137→    atomic_id = str(uuid4())
   138→
   139→    tree = None
   140→    if with_tree:
   141→        tree = {
   142→            "type": "ATOMIC",
   143→            "atomic_criterion_id": atomic_id,
   144→        }
   145→
   146→    crit = _make_criterion(
   147→        db_session,
   148→        batch.id,
   149→        criteria_type=criteria_type,
   150→        structured_criterion=tree,
   151→    )
   152→    atomic = _make_atomic(
   153→        db_session,
   154→        crit.id,
   155→        proto.id,
   156→        id=atomic_id,
   157→        inclusion_exclusion=criteria_type,
   158→        negation=negation,
   159→        entity_domain=entity_domain,
   160→    )
   161→    db_session.commit()
   162→
   163→    return ProtocolExportData(
   164→        protocol=proto,
   165→        criteria=[crit],
   166→        atomics=[atomic],
   167→        composites=[],
   168→        relationships=[],
   169→        atomics_by_id={atomic.id: atomic},
   170→        composites_by_id={},
   171→        children_by_parent={},
   172→        criteria_by_id={crit.id: crit},
   173→    )
   174→
   175→
   176→def _build_and_or_export_data(db_session):
   177→    """Build export data with AND containing two atomics and an OR group."""
   178→    proto = _make_protocol(db_session)
   179→    batch = _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)
   180→
   181→    a1_id = str(uuid4())
   182→    a2_id = str(uuid4())
   183→    a3_id = str(uuid4())
   184→
   185→    tree = {
   186→        "type": "AND",
   187→        "children": [
   188→            {"type": "ATOMIC", "atomic_criterion_id": a1_id},
   189→            {
   190→                "type": "OR",
   191→                "children": [
   192→                    {"type": "ATOMIC", "atomic_criterion_id": a2_id},
   193→                    {"type": "ATOMIC", "atomic_criterion_id": a3_id},
   194→                ],
   195→            },
   196→        ],
   197→    }
   198→
   199→    crit = _make_criterion(db_session, batch.id, structured_criterion=tree)
   200→
   201→    a1 = _make_atomic(
   202→        db_session,
   203→        crit.id,
   204→        proto.id,
   205→        id=a1_id,
   206→        omop_concept_id="201826",
   207→        entity_domain="condition",
   208→        relation_operator=None,
   209→        value_numeric=None,
   210→        unit_concept_id=None,
   211→        original_text="Type 2 diabetes",
   212→    )
   213→    a2 = _make_atomic(
   214→        db_session,
   215→        crit.id,
   216→        proto.id,
   217→        id=a2_id,
   218→        omop_concept_id="3004410",
   219→        entity_domain="measurement",
   220→        relation_operator=">=",
   221→        value_numeric=7.0,
   222→        original_text="HbA1c >= 7%",
   223→    )
   224→    a3 = _make_atomic(
   225→        db_session,
   226→        crit.id,
   227→        proto.id,
   228→        id=a3_id,
   229→        omop_concept_id="3000963",
   230→        entity_domain="measurement",
   231→        relation_operator=">=",
   232→        value_numeric=126.0,
   233→        original_text="FPG >= 126 mg/dL",
   234→    )
   235→    db_session.commit()
   236→
   237→    return ProtocolExportData(
   238→        protocol=proto,
   239→        criteria=[crit],
   240→        atomics=[a1, a2, a3],
   241→        composites=[],
   242→        relationships=[],
   243→        atomics_by_id={a1.id: a1, a2.id: a2, a3.id: a3},
   244→        composites_by_id={},
   245→        children_by_parent={},
   246→        criteria_by_id={crit.id: crit},
   247→    )
   248→

   251→# CIRCE Builder Unit Tests

   253→
   254→
   255→class TestCirceBuilder:
   256→    """Unit tests for the CIRCE CohortExpression builder."""
   257→
   258→    def test_concept_sets_created(self, db_session):
   259→        data = _build_simple_export_data(db_session)
   260→        result = build_circe_export(data)
   261→        assert len(result["ConceptSets"]) == 1
   262→        cs = result["ConceptSets"][0]
   263→        assert cs["expression"]["items"][0]["concept"]["CONCEPT_ID"] == 3004410
   264→        assert cs["expression"]["items"][0]["includeDescendants"] is True
   265→
   266→    def test_domain_mapping_measurement(self, db_session):
   267→        data = _build_simple_export_data(db_session, entity_domain="measurement")
   268→        result = build_circe_export(data)
   269→        criteria_list = result["AdditionalCriteria"]["CriteriaList"]
   270→        assert len(criteria_list) == 1
   271→        assert "Measurement" in criteria_list[0]["Criteria"]
   272→
   273→    def test_domain_mapping_condition(self, db_session):
   274→        data = _build_simple_export_data(db_session, entity_domain="condition")
   275→        result = build_circe_export(data)
   276→        criteria_list = result["AdditionalCriteria"]["CriteriaList"]
   277→        assert "ConditionOccurrence" in criteria_list[0]["Criteria"]
   278→
   279→    def test_value_and_unit_filter(self, db_session):
   280→        data = _build_simple_export_data(db_session)
   281→        result = build_circe_export(data)
   282→        criteria_entry = result["AdditionalCriteria"]["CriteriaList"][0]
   283→        val = criteria_entry["Criteria"]["ValueAsNumber"]
   284→        assert val["Value"] == 7.0
   285→        assert val["Op"] == "gte"
   286→        assert val["UnitConceptId"] == 8554
   287→
   288→    def test_negation_sets_occurrence_zero(self, db_session):
   289→        data = _build_simple_export_data(db_session, negation=True)
   290→        result = build_circe_export(data)
   291→        criteria_entry = result["AdditionalCriteria"]["CriteriaList"][0]
   292→        occ = criteria_entry["Criteria"]["OccurrenceCount"]
   293→        assert occ["Value"] == 0
   294→        assert occ["Op"] == "eq"
   295→
   296→    def test_exclusion_goes_to_censoring(self, db_session):
   297→        data = _build_simple_export_data(db_session, criteria_type="exclusion")
   298→        result = build_circe_export(data)
   299→        assert len(result["CensoringCriteria"]) == 1
   300→        assert len(result["AdditionalCriteria"]["CriteriaList"]) == 0
   301→
   302→    def test_and_or_groups(self, db_session):
   303→        data = _build_and_or_export_data(db_session)
   304→        result = build_circe_export(data)
   305→        criteria_list = result["AdditionalCriteria"]["CriteriaList"]
   306→        assert len(criteria_list) == 1
   307→        group = criteria_list[0]
   308→        assert group["Type"] == "ALL"
   309→        # AND group: first child is atomic, second is OR group
   310→        assert len(group["CriteriaList"]) == 2
   311→        or_group = group["CriteriaList"][1]
   312→        assert or_group["Type"] == "ANY"
   313→        assert len(or_group["CriteriaList"]) == 2
   314→
   315→    def test_omop_concept_id_precedence(self, db_session):
   316→        """omop_concept_id should be preferred over entity_concept_id."""
   317→        data = _build_simple_export_data(db_session)
   318→        result = build_circe_export(data)
   319→        # omop_concept_id is 3004410, entity_concept_id is 4532345
   320→        concept = result["ConceptSets"][0]["expression"]["items"][0]["concept"]
   321→        assert concept["CONCEPT_ID"] == 3004410
   322→
   323→    def test_empty_protocol_no_crash(self, db_session):
   324→        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)
   325→        batch = _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)
   326→        crit = _make_criterion(db_session, batch.id, structured_criterion=None)
   327→        db_session.commit()
   328→        data = ProtocolExportData(
   329→            protocol=proto,
   330→            criteria=[crit],
   331→            atomics=[],
   332→            composites=[],
   333→            relationships=[],
   334→            atomics_by_id={},
   335→            composites_by_id={},
   336→            children_by_parent={},
   337→            criteria_by_id={crit.id: crit},
   338→        )
   339→        result = build_circe_export(data)
   340→        assert result["ConceptSets"] == []
   341→

   348→class TestFhirGroupBuilder:
   349→    """Unit tests for the FHIR Group resource builder."""
   350→
   351→    def test_characteristics_created(self, db_session):
   352→        data = _build_simple_export_data(db_session)
   353→        result = build_fhir_group_export(data)
   354→        assert result["resourceType"] == "Group"
   355→        assert result["type"] == "person"
   356→        assert len(result["characteristic"]) == 1
   357→
   358→    def test_quantity_value(self, db_session):
   359→        data = _build_simple_export_data(db_session)
   360→        result = build_fhir_group_export(data)
   361→        char = result["characteristic"][0]
   362→        assert "valueQuantity" in char
   363→        assert char["valueQuantity"]["value"] == 7.0
   364→        assert char["valueQuantity"]["unit"] == "%"
   365→        assert char["valueQuantity"]["comparator"] == "ge"
   366→
   367→    def test_text_value(self, db_session):
   368→        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)
   369→        batch = _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)
   370→        a_id = str(uuid4())
   371→        tree = {"type": "ATOMIC", "atomic_criterion_id": a_id}
   372→        crit = _make_criterion(db_session, batch.id, structured_criterion=tree)
   373→        atomic = _make_atomic(
   374→            db_session,
   375→            crit.id,
   376→            proto.id,
   377→            id=a_id,
   378→            value_numeric=None,
   379→            value_text="positive",
   380→            relation_operator=None,
   381→            unit_text=None,
   382→            unit_concept_id=None,
   383→        )
   384→        db_session.commit()
   385→        data = ProtocolExportData(
   386→            protocol=proto,
   387→            criteria=[crit],
   388→            atomics=[atomic],
   389→            composites=[],
   390→            relationships=[],
   391→            atomics_by_id={atomic.id: atomic},
   392→            composites_by_id={},
   393→            children_by_parent={},
   394→            criteria_by_id={crit.id: crit},
   395→        )
   396→        result = build_fhir_group_export(data)
   397→        char = result["characteristic"][0]
   398→        assert "valueCodeableConcept" in char
   399→        assert char["valueCodeableConcept"]["text"] == "positive"
   400→
   401→    def test_exclusion_flag(self, db_session):
   402→        data = _build_simple_export_data(db_session, criteria_type="exclusion")
   403→        result = build_fhir_group_export(data)
   404→        char = result["characteristic"][0]
   405→        assert char["exclude"] is True
   406→
   407→    def test_or_nesting(self, db_session):
   408→        data = _build_and_or_export_data(db_session)
   409→        result = build_fhir_group_export(data)
   410→        chars = result["characteristic"]
   411→        # AND at top: first child is flat atomic, second is OR nested group
   412→        assert len(chars) == 2
   413→        or_char = chars[1]
   414→        assert "_contained" in or_char
   415→        nested = or_char["_contained"]
   416→        assert nested["resourceType"] == "Group"
   417→        assert any(e["valueCode"] == "any-of" for e in nested.get("extension", []))
   418→
   419→    def test_not_inverts_exclude(self, db_session):
   420→        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)
   421→        batch = _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)
   422→        a_id = str(uuid4())
   423→        tree = {
   424→            "type": "NOT",
   425→            "children": [
   426→                {"type": "ATOMIC", "atomic_criterion_id": a_id},
   427→            ],
   428→        }
   429→        crit = _make_criterion(
   430→            db_session,
   431→            batch.id,
   432→            criteria_type="inclusion",
   433→            structured_criterion=tree,
   434→        )
   435→        atomic = _make_atomic(
   436→            db_session,
   437→            crit.id,
   438→            proto.id,
   439→            id=a_id,
   440→            negation=False,
   441→        )
   442→        db_session.commit()
   443→        data = ProtocolExportData(
   444→            protocol=proto,
   445→            criteria=[crit],
   446→            atomics=[atomic],
   447→            composites=[],
   448→            relationships=[],
   449→            atomics_by_id={atomic.id: atomic},
   450→            composites_by_id={},
   451→            children_by_parent={},
   452→            criteria_by_id={crit.id: crit},
   453→        )
   454→        result = build_fhir_group_export(data)
   455→        # NOT on an inclusion criterion -> exclude=True
   456→        char = result["characteristic"][0]
   457→        assert char["exclude"] is True
   458→

   582→            atomics_by_id={},
   583→            composites_by_id={},
   584→            children_by_parent={},
   585→            criteria_by_id={},
   586→        )
   587→        sql = build_evaluation_sql(data)

   591→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   592→# Integration Tests (via test_client)
   593→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   594→
   595→
   596→class TestExportEndpoints:
   597→    """Integration tests for export API endpoints."""
   598→
   599→    def _setup_protocol_with_criteria(self, db_session):
   600→        """Create a full protocol with structured criteria."""
   601→        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)
   602→        batch = _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)
   603→        a_id = str(uuid4())
   604→        tree = {"type": "ATOMIC", "atomic_criterion_id": a_id}
   605→        crit = _make_criterion(db_session, batch.id, structured_criterion=tree)
   606→        _make_atomic(db_session, crit.id, proto.id, id=a_id)
   607→        db_session.commit()
   608→        return proto.id
   609→
   610→    def test_circe_200(self, test_client, db_session):
   611→        pid = self._setup_protocol_with_criteria(db_session)
   612→        r = test_client.get(f"/protocols/{pid}/export/circe")
   613→        assert r.status_code == 200
   614→        data = r.json()
   615→        assert "expression" in data
   616→        assert "stats" in data
   617→        assert data["stats"]["atomic_count"] == 1
   618→
   619→    def test_fhir_group_200(self, test_client, db_session):
   620→        pid = self._setup_protocol_with_criteria(db_session)
   621→        r = test_client.get(f"/protocols/{pid}/export/fhir-group")
   622→        assert r.status_code == 200
   623→        data = r.json()
   624→        assert "resource" in data
   625→        assert data["resource"]["resourceType"] == "Group"
   626→        assert data["stats"]["criteria_count"] == 1
   627→
   628→    def test_evaluation_sql_200(self, test_client, db_session):
   629→        pid = self._setup_protocol_with_criteria(db_session)
   630→        r = test_client.get(f"/protocols/{pid}/export/evaluation-sql")
   631→        assert r.status_code == 200
   632→        assert "SELECT p.person_id" in r.text
   633→
   634→    def test_circe_404_unknown_protocol(self, test_client):
   635→        r = test_client.get(f"/protocols/{uuid4()}/export/circe")
   636→        assert r.status_code == 404
   637→
   638→    def test_fhir_404_unknown_protocol(self, test_client):
   639→        r = test_client.get(f"/protocols/{uuid4()}/export/fhir-group")
   640→        assert r.status_code == 404
   641→
   642→    def test_sql_404_unknown_protocol(self, test_client):
   643→        r = test_client.get(f"/protocols/{uuid4()}/export/evaluation-sql")
   644→        assert r.status_code == 404
   645→
   646→    def test_404_no_structured_criteria(self, test_client, db_session):
   647→        """Protocol exists but batch has no criteria -> 404."""
   648→        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)
   649→        _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)
   650→        db_session.commit()
   651→        r = test_client.get(f"/protocols/{proto.id}/export/circe")
   652→        assert r.status_code == 404
   653→
   654→    def test_stats_match_data(self, test_client, db_session):
   655→        """Response stats should reflect actual data counts."""
   656→        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)
   657→        batch = _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)
   658→
   659→        a1_id = str(uuid4())
   660→        a2_id = str(uuid4())
   661→        tree = {
   662→            "type": "AND",
   663→            "children": [
   664→                {"type": "ATOMIC", "atomic_criterion_id": a1_id},
   665→                {"type": "ATOMIC", "atomic_criterion_id": a2_id},
   666→            ],
   667→        }
   668→        crit = _make_criterion(db_session, batch.id, structured_criterion=tree)
   669→        comp = _make_composite(db_session, crit.id, proto.id)
   670→        _make_atomic(db_session, crit.id, proto.id, id=a1_id)
   671→        _make_atomic(
   672→            db_session,
   673→            crit.id,
   674→            proto.id,
   675→            id=a2_id,
   676→            omop_concept_id="201826",
   677→            entity_domain="condition",
   678→        )
   679→        _make_relationship(db_session, comp.id, a1_id, "atomic", 0)
   680→        _make_relationship(db_session, comp.id, a2_id, "atomic", 1)
   681→        db_session.commit()
   682→
   683→        r = test_client.get(f"/protocols/{proto.id}/export/circe")
   684→        assert r.status_code == 200
   685→        stats = r.json()["stats"]
   686→        assert stats["criteria_count"] == 1
   687→        assert stats["atomic_count"] == 2
   688→        assert stats["composite_count"] == 1
   689→        assert stats["relationship_count"] == 2
   690→
   691→    def test_auth_required(self, unauthenticated_client, db_session):
   692→        """Export endpoints require authentication."""
   693→        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)
   694→        db_session.commit()
   695→        r = unauthenticated_client.get(f"/protocols/{proto.id}/export/circe")
   696→        assert r.status_code == 401
   697→
```

```json
  "new_string": "    def test_omop_concept_id_precedence(self, db_session):\n        \"\"\"omop_concept_id should be preferred over entity_concept_id.\"\"\"\n        data = _build_simple_export_data(db_session)\n        result = build_circe_export(data)\n        # omop_concept_id is 3004410, entity_concept_id is 4532345\n        concept = result[\"ConceptSets\"][0][\"expression\"][\"items\"][0][\"concept\"]\n        assert concept[\"CONCEPT_ID\"] == 3004410\n\n    def test_demographics_uses_demographic_criteria(self, db_session):\n        \"\"\"Demographics domain should produce DemographicCriteria, not a ConceptSet.\"\"\"\n        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)\n        batch = _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)\n        a_id = str(uuid4())\n        tree = {\"type\": \"ATOMIC\", \"atomic_criterion_id\": a_id}\n        crit = _make_criterion(db_session, batch.id, structured_criterion=tree)\n        _make_atomic(\n            db_session,\n            crit.id,\n            proto.id,\n            id=a_id,\n            entity_domain=\"demographics\",\n            relation_operator=\">=\",\n            value_numeric=18.0,\n            unit_text=\"years\",\n            unit_concept_id=None,\n            original_text=\"Age >= 18 years\",\n        )\n        db_session.commit()\n        data = ProtocolExportData(\n            protocol=proto,\n            criteria=[crit],\n            atomics=[db_session.get(AtomicCriterion, a_id)],\n            composites=[],\n            relationships=[],\n            atomics_by_id={a_id: db_session.get(AtomicCriterion, a_id)},\n            composites_by_id={},\n            children_by_parent={},\n            criteria_by_id={crit.id: crit},\n        )\n        result = build_circe_export(data)\n        # No ConceptSets for demographics\n        assert len(result[\"ConceptSets\"]) == 0\n        criteria_list = result[\"AdditionalCriteria\"][\"CriteriaList\"]\n        assert len(criteria_list) == 1\n        entry = criteria_list[0]\n        assert \"DemographicCriteria\" in entry[\"Criteria\"]\n        age = entry[\"Criteria\"][\"DemographicCriteria\"][\"Age\"]\n        assert age[\"Value\"] == 18\n        assert age[\"Op\"] == \"gte\"\n\n    def test_empty_protocol_no_crash(self, db_session):"
```

```json
  "new_string": "    def test_demographics_uses_age_context_type(self, db_session):\n        \"\"\"Demographics domain should use usage-context-type|age, not SNOMED.\"\"\"\n        proto = _make_protocol(db_session)\n        batch = _make_batch(db_session, proto.id)\n        a_id = str(uuid4())\n        tree = {\"type\": \"ATOMIC\", \"atomic_criterion_id\": a_id}\n        crit = _make_criterion(db_session, batch.id, structured_criterion=tree)\n        _make_atomic(\n            db_session,\n            crit.id,\n            proto.id,\n            id=a_id,\n            entity_domain=\"demographics\",\n            relation_operator=\">=\",\n            value_numeric=18.0,\n            unit_text=\"years\",\n            unit_concept_id=None,\n            original_text=\"Age >= 18 years\",\n        )\n        db_session.commit()\n        data = ProtocolExportData(\n            protocol=proto,\n            criteria=[crit],\n            atomics=[db_session.get(AtomicCriterion, a_id)],\n            composites=[],\n            relationships=[],\n            atomics_by_id={a_id: db_session.get(AtomicCriterion, a_id)},\n            composites_by_id={},\n            children_by_parent={},\n            criteria_by_id={crit.id: crit},\n        )\n        result = build_fhir_group_export(data)\n        char = result[\"characteristic\"][0]\n        coding = char[\"code\"][\"coding\"][0]\n        assert coding[\"system\"] == (\n            \"http://terminology.hl7.org/CodeSystem/usage-context-type\"\n        )\n        assert coding[\"code\"] == \"age\"\n        assert \"valueQuantity\" in char\n        q = char[\"valueQuantity\"]\n        assert q[\"value\"] == 18.0\n        assert q[\"unit\"] == \"years\"\n        assert q[\"system\"] == \"http://unitsofmeasure.org\"\n        assert q[\"comparator\"] == \"ge\"\n\n    def test_system_uri_loinc(self, db_session):"
}
```

```
========================================================================
  OMOP CDM v5.4 Evaluation SQL
========================================================================
-- Auto-generated OMOP CDM v5.4 eligibility evaluation SQL
-- Protocol: Phase 3 T2DM SGLT2 Inhibitor Trial (NCT00000000) (3ea8cc6f-4d92-4afc-bb37-0b6cc1167837)
-- Generated from 9 atomic criteria

WITH
cte_0 AS (
    SELECT p.person_id
    FROM person p
    WHERE EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE) - p.year_of_birth >= 18
),

cte_1 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM condition_occurrence t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.condition_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 201826
),

cte_2 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM measurement t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.measurement_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 3004410
    AND t.value_as_number >= 7.0
    AND t.unit_concept_id = 8554
),

cte_3 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM measurement t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.measurement_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 3004410
    AND t.value_as_number <= 10.0
    AND t.unit_concept_id = 8554
),

cte_4 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM measurement t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.measurement_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 3049187
    AND t.value_as_number >= 30.0
    AND t.unit_concept_id = 8795
),

cte_5 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM condition_occurrence t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.condition_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 201254
),

cte_6 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM drug_exposure t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.drug_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 1567198
),

cte_7 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM drug_exposure t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.drug_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 1583722
),

cte_8 AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT t.person_id
    FROM condition_occurrence t
    INNER JOIN concept_ancestor ca
        ON ca.descendant_concept_id = t.condition_concept_id
    WHERE ca.ancestor_concept_id = 316139
)


SELECT p.person_id
FROM person p
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_0 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_1 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_2 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_3 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_4 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_5 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_6 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_7 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM cte_8 c
    WHERE c.person_id = p.person_id
)
;

========================================================================
  DONE
========================================================================
```

- **FHIR**: Age characteristic uses `usage-context-type|age` code system with `valueQuantity` (UCUM unit "a")  
- **SQL**: Age uses `EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE) - p.year_of_birth >= 18`

```
### **Phase 4: Join-Ready Export (CIRCE, FHIR Group, Evaluation SQL)**
```
